module;

#include <cstdint>
#include <optional>
#include <vector>

export module dgtl.mir.machine_ir:allocation;

import :types;
import :target;
import :function;

export namespace dgtl::machine {

/* ============================================================================
 * Liveness / live intervals
 * ========================================================================== */

struct MachinePosition {
    InstrId instr = INVALID_INSTR_ID;
    std::uint32_t slot = 0;

    friend constexpr bool operator<(const MachinePosition& a,
                                    const MachinePosition& b) noexcept {
        return a.instr < b.instr ||
               (a.instr == b.instr && a.slot < b.slot);
    }

    friend constexpr bool operator==(const MachinePosition&,
                                     const MachinePosition&) noexcept = default;
};

struct MachineLiveRange {
    MachinePosition start = {};
    MachinePosition end = {};
    bool valid = false;

    [[nodiscard]] bool contains(MachinePosition position) const noexcept {
        return valid && !(position < start) && !(end < position);
    }
};

struct MachineLiveInterval {
    MachineRegister reg = {};
    std::vector<MachineLiveRange> ranges;
    std::vector<MachineRegister> uses;
    std::vector<MachineRegister> defs;

    bool spillable = true;
    bool rematerializable = false;
    bool fixed = false;
};

class MachineLiveness {
public:
    virtual ~MachineLiveness() = default;

    [[nodiscard]] virtual const MachineLiveInterval*
    interval(MachineRegister reg) const noexcept = 0;

    [[nodiscard]] virtual bool
    is_live_at(MachineRegister reg,
               MachinePosition position) const noexcept = 0;

    [[nodiscard]] virtual bool
    interferes(MachineRegister lhs,
               MachineRegister rhs) const noexcept = 0;
};

/* ============================================================================
 * Register Allocator & Spiller contracts
 * ========================================================================== */

enum class AllocationResultKind : std::uint8_t {
    Assigned,
    Spilled,
    Rejected
};

struct RegisterAssignment {
    MachineRegister virtual_reg = {};
    MachineRegister physical_reg = {};
    AllocationResultKind result = AllocationResultKind::Rejected;
    StackSlotId spill_slot = INVALID_STACK_SLOT;
};

class MachineRegisterAllocator {
public:
    virtual ~MachineRegisterAllocator() = default;

    virtual bool run() = 0;

    [[nodiscard]] virtual std::optional<MachineRegister>
    assigned_register(MachineRegister virtual_reg) const noexcept = 0;

    [[nodiscard]] virtual std::optional<StackSlotId>
    spill_slot(MachineRegister virtual_reg) const noexcept = 0;
};

struct SpillRequest {
    MachineRegister virtual_reg = {};
    RegisterClassKind reg_class = RegisterClassKind::Invalid;
    std::uint32_t size_bytes = 0;
    std::uint32_t alignment = 1;
};

struct SpillResult {
    bool spilled = false;
    StackSlotId slot = INVALID_STACK_SLOT;
    std::vector<InstrId> inserted_instructions;
};

class MachineSpiller {
public:
    virtual ~MachineSpiller() = default;

    [[nodiscard]] virtual SpillResult
    spill(MachineFunction& function,
          const SpillRequest& request) = 0;

    virtual bool rewrite_for_spill(MachineFunction& function,
                                   MachineRegister virtual_reg,
                                   StackSlotId slot) = 0;
};

/* ============================================================================
 * Unified Machine Compilation Context
 * ========================================================================== */

struct MachineContext {
    const TargetRegisterInfo* register_info = nullptr;
    const TargetInstructionInfo* instruction_info = nullptr;
    const TargetFrameInfo* frame_info = nullptr;
    const TargetABI* abi = nullptr;
    MachineLiveness* liveness = nullptr;
    MachineRegisterAllocator* register_allocator = nullptr;
    MachineSpiller* spiller = nullptr;
    MachineEmitter* emitter = nullptr;

    [[nodiscard]] bool complete_for_allocation() const noexcept {
        return register_info != nullptr &&
               instruction_info != nullptr &&
               frame_info != nullptr &&
               abi != nullptr &&
               liveness != nullptr &&
               register_allocator != nullptr;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool complete_for_emission() const noexcept {
        return complete_for_allocation() &&
               emitter != nullptr;
    }
};

} // namespace dgtl::machine

module;

#include <algorithm>
#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <optional>
#include <span>
#include <utility>
#include <vector>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <queue>
#include <stack>

export module dgtl.mir.machine_ir:analysis;

import :types;
import :target;
import :function;

export namespace dgtl::machine {

enum class MachineLoopKind : std::uint8_t {
    For,
    While,
    DoWhile,
    Infinite
};

struct MachineInductionVariable {
    MachineRegister iv_reg{};
    std::int64_t init_value = 0;
    std::int64_t step_value = 1;
    std::int64_t limit_value = 0;
    MachineSemanticKind compare_kind = MachineSemanticKind::Compare;
    bool is_constant_trip_count = false;
    std::uint64_t trip_count = 0;
};

class MachineLoop {
public:
    BlockId header = INVALID_BLOCK_ID;
    BlockId preheader = INVALID_BLOCK_ID;
    BlockId latch = INVALID_BLOCK_ID;
    std::vector<BlockId> latches;
    std::vector<BlockId> blocks;
    std::vector<BlockId> exit_blocks;
    std::vector<BlockId> exiting_blocks;
    MachineLoopKind kind = MachineLoopKind::While;
    std::optional<MachineInductionVariable> induction_var;
    std::uint32_t depth = 1;

    [[nodiscard]] bool contains(BlockId bb) const noexcept {
        return std::find(blocks.begin(), blocks.end(), bb) != blocks.end();
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool contains(const MachineLoop& subloop) const noexcept {
        for (BlockId b : subloop.blocks) {
            if (!contains(b)) return false;
        }
        return true;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool is_loop_header(BlockId bb) const noexcept {
        return header == bb;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool is_loop_latch(BlockId bb) const noexcept {
        return latch == bb || std::find(latches.begin(), latches.end(), bb) != latches.end();
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool is_loop_exiting(BlockId bb) const noexcept {
        return std::find(exiting_blocks.begin(), exiting_blocks.end(), bb) != exiting_blocks.end();
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool is_loop_exit(BlockId bb) const noexcept {
        return std::find(exit_blocks.begin(), exit_blocks.end(), bb) != exit_blocks.end();
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool is_for_loop() const noexcept { return kind == MachineLoopKind::For; }
    [[nodiscard]] bool is_while_loop() const noexcept { return kind == MachineLoopKind::While; }
    [[nodiscard]] bool is_do_while_loop() const noexcept { return kind == MachineLoopKind::DoWhile; }

    [[nodiscard]] bool has_constant_trip_count() const noexcept {
        return induction_var && induction_var->is_constant_trip_count;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] std::uint64_t trip_count() const noexcept {
        return induction_var ? induction_var->trip_count : 0;
    }
};

struct MachineDomTreeNode {
    BlockId block = INVALID_BLOCK_ID;
    BlockId idom = INVALID_BLOCK_ID;
    std::vector<BlockId> children;
    std::uint32_t dfs_in = 0;
    std::uint32_t dfs_out = 0;
    std::uint32_t level = 0;
};

class MachineDominatorTree {
    BlockId root_ = INVALID_BLOCK_ID;
    std::vector<MachineDomTreeNode> nodes_;

public:
    void clear() noexcept {
        root_ = INVALID_BLOCK_ID;
        nodes_.clear();
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool empty() const noexcept {
        return root_ == INVALID_BLOCK_ID || nodes_.empty();
    }

    [[nodiscard]] BlockId root() const noexcept {
        return root_;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] std::size_t size() const noexcept {
        return nodes_.size();
    }

    [[nodiscard]] const std::vector<MachineDomTreeNode>& nodes() const noexcept {
        return nodes_;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] const MachineDomTreeNode* node(BlockId b) const noexcept {
        if (b >= nodes_.size()) return nullptr;
        return &nodes_[b];
    }

    [[nodiscard]] BlockId idom(BlockId b) const noexcept {
        if (b >= nodes_.size()) return INVALID_BLOCK_ID;
        return nodes_[b].idom;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool is_reachable(BlockId b) const noexcept {
        if (b >= nodes_.size()) return false;
        return nodes_[b].dfs_in > 0;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] std::span<const BlockId> children(BlockId b) const noexcept {
        if (b >= nodes_.size()) return {};
        return nodes_[b].children;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] std::uint32_t level(BlockId b) const noexcept {
        if (b >= nodes_.size()) return 0;
        return nodes_[b].level;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool dominates(BlockId a, BlockId b) const noexcept {
        if (a == b) return true;
        if (a >= nodes_.size() || b >= nodes_.size()) return false;
        const auto& na = nodes_[a];
        const auto& nb = nodes_[b];
        if (na.dfs_in == 0 || nb.dfs_in == 0) return false;
        return (na.dfs_in <= nb.dfs_in) && (na.dfs_out >= nb.dfs_out);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool strictly_dominates(BlockId a, BlockId b) const noexcept {
        return (a != b) && dominates(a, b);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] BlockId nearest_common_dominator(BlockId a, BlockId b) const noexcept {
        if (a == b) return a;
        if (a >= nodes_.size() || b >= nodes_.size()) return INVALID_BLOCK_ID;
        if (dominates(a, b)) return a;
        if (dominates(b, a)) return b;

        BlockId curr_a = a;
        BlockId curr_b = b;
        while (curr_a != INVALID_BLOCK_ID && curr_b != INVALID_BLOCK_ID && curr_a != curr_b) {
            if (nodes_[curr_a].level > nodes_[curr_b].level) {
                curr_a = nodes_[curr_a].idom;
            } else if (nodes_[curr_b].level > nodes_[curr_a].level) {
                curr_b = nodes_[curr_b].idom;
            } else {
                curr_a = nodes_[curr_a].idom;
                curr_b = nodes_[curr_b].idom;
            }
        }
        return curr_a;
    }

    void compute(const MachineFunction& mf);
};

class MachineDominanceFrontier {
    std::vector<std::vector<BlockId>> frontiers_;

public:
    void clear() noexcept {
        frontiers_.clear();
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool empty() const noexcept {
        return frontiers_.empty();
    }

    [[nodiscard]] std::span<const BlockId> get_frontier(BlockId b) const noexcept {
        if (b >= frontiers_.size()) return {};
        return frontiers_[b];
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool in_frontier(BlockId a, BlockId b) const noexcept {
        if (a >= frontiers_.size()) return false;
        const auto& f = frontiers_[a];
        return std::find(f.begin(), f.end(), b) != f.end();
    }

    [[nodiscard]] std::vector<BlockId> compute_iterated_dominance_frontier(
        std::span<const BlockId> def_blocks) const;

    void compute(const MachineFunction& mf, const MachineDominatorTree& dom_tree);
};

class MachineLoopInfo {
    std::vector<MachineLoop> loops_;
    std::unordered_map<BlockId, std::size_t> block_to_loop_;

public:
    void clear() noexcept {
        loops_.clear();
        block_to_loop_.clear();
    }

    [[nodiscard]] const std::vector<MachineLoop>& loops() const noexcept {
        return loops_;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] const MachineLoop* get_loop_for(BlockId bb) const noexcept {
        auto it = block_to_loop_.find(bb);
        if (it != block_to_loop_.end() && it->second < loops_.size()) {
            return &loops_[it->second];
        }
        return nullptr;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] std::uint32_t get_loop_depth(BlockId bb) const noexcept {
        const auto* l = get_loop_for(bb);
        return l ? l->depth : 0;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool is_loop_header(BlockId bb) const noexcept {
        const auto* l = get_loop_for(bb);
        return l && l->header == bb;
    }

    void add_loop(MachineLoop loop) {
        const std::size_t idx = loops_.size();
        for (BlockId bb : loop.blocks) {
            block_to_loop_[bb] = idx;
        }
        loops_.push_back(std::move(loop));
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool empty() const noexcept { return loops_.empty(); }
    [[nodiscard]] std::size_t size() const noexcept { return loops_.size(); }

    void compute(const MachineFunction& mf, const MachineDominatorTree& dom_tree);
};

/* ============================================================================
 * Algorithm Implementations
 * ========================================================================== */

inline void MachineDominatorTree::compute(const MachineFunction& mf) {
    clear();
    const auto block_span = mf.blocks();
    const std::size_t n = block_span.size();
    if (n == 0) return;

    root_ = mf.entry_block();
    if (root_ == INVALID_BLOCK_ID || root_ >= n) return;

    nodes_.resize(n);
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        nodes_[i].block = static_cast<BlockId>(i);
        nodes_[i].idom = INVALID_BLOCK_ID;
        nodes_[i].children.clear();
        nodes_[i].dfs_in = 0;
        nodes_[i].dfs_out = 0;
        nodes_[i].level = 0;
    }

    std::vector<bool> visited(n, false);
    std::vector<BlockId> post_order;
    post_order.reserve(n);
    std::vector<std::int32_t> post_order_num(n, -1);

    std::vector<std::pair<BlockId, std::size_t>> dfs_stack;
    visited[root_] = true;
    dfs_stack.push_back({root_, 0});

    while (!dfs_stack.empty()) {
        auto& [u, succ_idx] = dfs_stack.back();
        const auto* bb = mf.block(u);
        if (bb && succ_idx < bb->successors.size()) {
            BlockId succ = bb->successors[succ_idx++];
            if (succ < n && !visited[succ]) {
                visited[succ] = true;
                dfs_stack.push_back({succ, 0});
            }
        } else {
            post_order_num[u] = static_cast<std::int32_t>(post_order.size());
            post_order.push_back(u);
            dfs_stack.pop_back();
        }
    }

    std::vector<BlockId> idoms(n, INVALID_BLOCK_ID);
    idoms[root_] = root_;

    auto intersect = [&](BlockId b1, BlockId b2) -> BlockId {
        BlockId finger1 = b1;
        BlockId finger2 = b2;
        while (finger1 != finger2) {
            while (finger1 != INVALID_BLOCK_ID && finger2 != INVALID_BLOCK_ID &&
                   post_order_num[finger1] < post_order_num[finger2]) {
                finger1 = idoms[finger1];
            }
            while (finger1 != INVALID_BLOCK_ID && finger2 != INVALID_BLOCK_ID &&
                   post_order_num[finger2] < post_order_num[finger1]) {
                finger2 = idoms[finger2];
            }
            if (finger1 == INVALID_BLOCK_ID || finger2 == INVALID_BLOCK_ID) {
                break;
            }
        }
        return (finger1 != INVALID_BLOCK_ID) ? finger1 : finger2;
    };

    bool changed = true;
    while (changed) {
        changed = false;
        for (auto it = post_order.rbegin(); it != post_order.rend(); ++it) {
            const BlockId b = *it;
            if (b == root_) continue;
            const auto* bb = mf.block(b);
            if (!bb) continue;

            BlockId new_idom = INVALID_BLOCK_ID;
            for (BlockId p : bb->predecessors) {
                if (p < n && idoms[p] != INVALID_BLOCK_ID) {
                    new_idom = p;
                    break;
                }
            }
            if (new_idom == INVALID_BLOCK_ID) continue;

            for (BlockId p : bb->predecessors) {
                if (p < n && p != new_idom && idoms[p] != INVALID_BLOCK_ID) {
                    new_idom = intersect(p, new_idom);
                }
            }

            if (idoms[b] != new_idom) {
                idoms[b] = new_idom;
                changed = true;
            }
        }
    }
    idoms[root_] = INVALID_BLOCK_ID;

    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        nodes_[i].idom = idoms[i];
        if (idoms[i] != INVALID_BLOCK_ID && idoms[i] < n) {
            nodes_[idoms[i]].children.push_back(static_cast<BlockId>(i));
        }
    }

    std::uint32_t timestamp = 0;
    nodes_[root_].level = 0;
    nodes_[root_].dfs_in = ++timestamp;

    std::vector<std::pair<BlockId, std::size_t>> dom_stack;
    dom_stack.push_back({root_, 0});

    while (!dom_stack.empty()) {
        auto& [u, child_idx] = dom_stack.back();
        if (child_idx < nodes_[u].children.size()) {
            BlockId v = nodes_[u].children[child_idx++];
            nodes_[v].level = nodes_[u].level + 1;
            nodes_[v].dfs_in = ++timestamp;
            dom_stack.push_back({v, 0});
        } else {
            nodes_[u].dfs_out = ++timestamp;
            dom_stack.pop_back();
        }
    }
}

inline void MachineDominanceFrontier::compute(const MachineFunction& mf, const MachineDominatorTree& dom_tree) {
    clear();
    const auto n = mf.blocks().size();
    frontiers_.assign(n, {});
    if (n == 0 || dom_tree.empty()) return;

    for (const auto& bb : mf.blocks()) {
        if (bb.predecessors.size() < 2) continue;
        const BlockId b = bb.id;
        const BlockId idom_b = dom_tree.idom(b);

        for (BlockId p : bb.predecessors) {
            BlockId runner = p;
            while (runner != INVALID_BLOCK_ID && runner != idom_b && runner < n) {
                auto& df = frontiers_[runner];
                if (std::find(df.begin(), df.end(), b) == df.end()) {
                    df.push_back(b);
                }
                runner = dom_tree.idom(runner);
            }
        }
    }
}

inline std::vector<BlockId> MachineDominanceFrontier::compute_iterated_dominance_frontier(
    std::span<const BlockId> def_blocks) const {
    std::vector<BlockId> idf;
    if (def_blocks.empty() || frontiers_.empty()) return idf;

    std::vector<bool> in_idf(frontiers_.size(), false);
    std::vector<bool> in_worklist(frontiers_.size(), false);
    std::vector<BlockId> worklist;
    worklist.reserve(def_blocks.size());

    for (BlockId b : def_blocks) {
        if (b < frontiers_.size()) {
            worklist.push_back(b);
            in_worklist[b] = true;
        }
    }

    std::size_t idx = 0;
    while (idx < worklist.size()) {
        BlockId node = worklist[idx++];
        if (node >= frontiers_.size()) continue;

        for (BlockId frontier_node : frontiers_[node]) {
            if (frontier_node < frontiers_.size() && !in_idf[frontier_node]) {
                in_idf[frontier_node] = true;
                idf.push_back(frontier_node);
                if (!in_worklist[frontier_node]) {
                    in_worklist[frontier_node] = true;
                    worklist.push_back(frontier_node);
                }
            }
        }
    }
    return idf;
}

inline void MachineLoopInfo::compute(const MachineFunction& mf, const MachineDominatorTree& dom_tree) {
    clear();
    const auto block_count = mf.blocks().size();
    if (block_count == 0 || dom_tree.empty()) return;

    std::unordered_map<BlockId, std::vector<BlockId>> header_to_latches;
    for (const auto& bb : mf.blocks()) {
        const BlockId u = bb.id;
        for (BlockId v : bb.successors) {
            if (dom_tree.dominates(v, u)) {
                header_to_latches[v].push_back(u);
            }
        }
    }

    for (auto& [header, latches] : header_to_latches) {
        MachineLoop loop;
        loop.header = header;
        loop.latch = latches.front();
        loop.latches = latches;

        std::vector<bool> in_loop(block_count, false);
        loop.blocks.push_back(header);
        in_loop[header] = true;

        std::vector<BlockId> worklist;
        for (BlockId latch : latches) {
            if (!in_loop[latch]) {
                in_loop[latch] = true;
                loop.blocks.push_back(latch);
            }
            worklist.push_back(latch);
        }

        while (!worklist.empty()) {
            BlockId curr = worklist.back();
            worklist.pop_back();
            const auto* curr_bb = mf.block(curr);
            if (!curr_bb) continue;

            for (BlockId pred : curr_bb->predecessors) {
                if (pred < block_count && !in_loop[pred]) {
                    in_loop[pred] = true;
                    loop.blocks.push_back(pred);
                    worklist.push_back(pred);
                }
            }
        }

        const auto* header_bb = mf.block(header);
        if (header_bb) {
            BlockId candidate_preheader = INVALID_BLOCK_ID;
            std::size_t outside_preds = 0;
            for (BlockId pred : header_bb->predecessors) {
                if (pred < block_count && !in_loop[pred]) {
                    outside_preds++;
                    candidate_preheader = pred;
                }
            }
            if (outside_preds == 1) {
                loop.preheader = candidate_preheader;
            }
        }

        for (BlockId b : loop.blocks) {
            const auto* b_bb = mf.block(b);
            if (!b_bb) continue;
            bool is_exiting = false;
            for (BlockId succ : b_bb->successors) {
                if (succ < block_count && !in_loop[succ]) {
                    is_exiting = true;
                    if (std::find(loop.exit_blocks.begin(), loop.exit_blocks.end(), succ) == loop.exit_blocks.end()) {
                        loop.exit_blocks.push_back(succ);
                    }
                }
            }
            if (is_exiting) {
                loop.exiting_blocks.push_back(b);
            }
        }

        loop.kind = MachineLoopKind::While;
        const auto* latch_bb = mf.block(loop.latch);
        if (latch_bb && !latch_bb->instructions.empty()) {
            const auto* last_inst = mf.instruction(latch_bb->instructions.back());
            if (last_inst && (last_inst->form == MachineForm::BranchCond || last_inst->form == MachineForm::BranchCompare)) {
                loop.kind = MachineLoopKind::DoWhile;
            }
        }

        add_loop(std::move(loop));
    }

    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < loops_.size(); ++i) {
        std::uint32_t depth = 1;
        for (std::size_t j = 0; j < loops_.size(); ++j) {
            if (i == j) continue;
            if (loops_[j].blocks.size() > loops_[i].blocks.size() &&
                loops_[j].contains(loops_[i].header)) {
                depth++;
            }
        }
        loops_[i].depth = depth;
    }

    block_to_loop_.clear();
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < loops_.size(); ++i) {
        for (BlockId bb : loops_[i].blocks) {
            auto it = block_to_loop_.find(bb);
            if (it == block_to_loop_.end()) {
                block_to_loop_[bb] = i;
            } else {
                if (loops_[i].depth > loops_[it->second].depth) {
                    block_to_loop_[bb] = i;
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

// Concrete bridge method implementations for MachineFunction
inline void MachineFunction::invalidate_analysis_caches() noexcept {
    dom_tree_valid_ = false;
    dom_frontier_valid_ = false;
    loop_info_valid_ = false;
}

inline void MachineFunction::insert_instruction_before(BlockId block_id, InstrId cursor_id, InstrId new_instr_id) {
    MachineBasicBlock* bb = block(block_id);
    MachineInstruction* new_inst = instruction(new_instr_id);
    if (!bb || !new_inst) return;
    if (new_inst->parent_block != INVALID_BLOCK_ID) return;

    if (cursor_id != INVALID_INSTR_ID) {
        MachineInstruction* cursor = instruction(cursor_id);
        if (!cursor || cursor->parent_block != block_id) return;
    }

    invalidate_analysis_caches();
    new_inst->parent_block = block_id;
    if (cursor_id == INVALID_INSTR_ID || bb->head_instr == INVALID_INSTR_ID) {
        if (bb->tail_instr != INVALID_INSTR_ID) {
            instruction(bb->tail_instr)->next_instr = new_instr_id;
            new_inst->prev_instr = bb->tail_instr;
            new_inst->next_instr = INVALID_INSTR_ID;
            bb->tail_instr = new_instr_id;
        } else {
            bb->head_instr = bb->tail_instr = new_instr_id;
            new_inst->prev_instr = new_inst->next_instr = INVALID_INSTR_ID;
        }
        bb->instructions.push_back(new_instr_id);
    } else {
        MachineInstruction* cursor = instruction(cursor_id);
        new_inst->next_instr = cursor_id;
        new_inst->prev_instr = cursor->prev_instr;
        if (cursor->prev_instr != INVALID_INSTR_ID) {
            instruction(cursor->prev_instr)->next_instr = new_instr_id;
        } else {
            bb->head_instr = new_instr_id;
        }
        cursor->prev_instr = new_instr_id;

        const auto it = std::find(bb->instructions.begin(), bb->instructions.end(), cursor_id);
        if (it == bb->instructions.end()) return;
        bb->instructions.insert(it, new_instr_id);
    }
    bb->instr_count = bb->instructions.size();
}

inline void MachineFunction::remove_instruction(InstrId instr_id) {
    MachineInstruction* inst = instruction(instr_id);
    if (!inst || inst->parent_block == INVALID_BLOCK_ID) return;
    MachineBasicBlock* bb = block(inst->parent_block);
    if (!bb) return;

    invalidate_analysis_caches();

    if (inst->prev_instr != INVALID_INSTR_ID) {
        MachineInstruction* prev = instruction(inst->prev_instr);
        if (prev) prev->next_instr = inst->next_instr;
    } else {
        bb->head_instr = inst->next_instr;
    }

    if (inst->next_instr != INVALID_INSTR_ID) {
        MachineInstruction* next = instruction(inst->next_instr);
        if (next) next->prev_instr = inst->prev_instr;
    } else {
        bb->tail_instr = inst->prev_instr;
    }

    auto it = std::find(bb->instructions.begin(), bb->instructions.end(), instr_id);
    if (it != bb->instructions.end()) {
        bb->instructions.erase(it);
    }
    bb->instr_count = bb->instructions.size();

    inst->parent_block = INVALID_BLOCK_ID;
    inst->prev_instr = INVALID_INSTR_ID;
    inst->next_instr = INVALID_INSTR_ID;
}

inline void MachineFunction::splice_instructions(BlockId dest_block_id, InstrId cursor_id,
                                                BlockId src_block_id, InstrId first_instr_id, InstrId last_instr_id) {
    if (src_block_id == dest_block_id && first_instr_id == cursor_id) return;
    MachineBasicBlock* src_bb = block(src_block_id);
    MachineBasicBlock* dest_bb = block(dest_block_id);
    if (!src_bb || !dest_bb) return;

    MachineInstruction* first_inst = instruction(first_instr_id);
    MachineInstruction* last_inst = instruction(last_instr_id);
    if (!first_inst || !last_inst) return;
    if (first_inst->parent_block != src_block_id || last_inst->parent_block != src_block_id) return;

    invalidate_analysis_caches();

    std::vector<InstrId> range;
    InstrId cur = first_instr_id;
    while (cur != INVALID_INSTR_ID) {
        range.push_back(cur);
        MachineInstruction* ci = instruction(cur);
        if (!ci) break;
        ci->parent_block = dest_block_id;
        if (cur == last_instr_id) break;
        cur = ci->next_instr;
    }

    if (first_inst->prev_instr != INVALID_INSTR_ID) {
        MachineInstruction* prev = instruction(first_inst->prev_instr);
        if (prev) prev->next_instr = last_inst->next_instr;
    } else {
        src_bb->head_instr = last_inst->next_instr;
    }

    if (last_inst->next_instr != INVALID_INSTR_ID) {
        MachineInstruction* next = instruction(last_inst->next_instr);
        if (next) next->prev_instr = first_inst->prev_instr;
    } else {
        src_bb->tail_instr = first_inst->prev_instr;
    }

    for (InstrId id : range) {
        auto it = std::find(src_bb->instructions.begin(), src_bb->instructions.end(), id);
        if (it != src_bb->instructions.end()) {
            src_bb->instructions.erase(it);
        }
    }
    src_bb->instr_count = src_bb->instructions.size();

    if (cursor_id == INVALID_INSTR_ID || dest_bb->head_instr == INVALID_INSTR_ID) {
        if (dest_bb->tail_instr != INVALID_INSTR_ID) {
            instruction(dest_bb->tail_instr)->next_instr = first_instr_id;
            first_inst->prev_instr = dest_bb->tail_instr;
            last_inst->next_instr = INVALID_INSTR_ID;
            dest_bb->tail_instr = last_instr_id;
        } else {
            dest_bb->head_instr = first_instr_id;
            dest_bb->tail_instr = last_instr_id;
            first_inst->prev_instr = INVALID_INSTR_ID;
            last_inst->next_instr = INVALID_INSTR_ID;
        }
        dest_bb->instructions.insert(dest_bb->instructions.end(), range.begin(), range.end());
    } else {
        MachineInstruction* cursor = instruction(cursor_id);
        first_inst->prev_instr = cursor->prev_instr;
        first_inst->next_instr = (first_instr_id == last_instr_id) ? cursor_id : first_inst->next_instr;
        last_inst->next_instr = cursor_id;

        if (cursor->prev_instr != INVALID_INSTR_ID) {
            instruction(cursor->prev_instr)->next_instr = first_instr_id;
        } else {
            dest_bb->head_instr = first_instr_id;
        }
        cursor->prev_instr = last_instr_id;

        auto it = std::find(dest_bb->instructions.begin(), dest_bb->instructions.end(), cursor_id);
        if (it != dest_bb->instructions.end()) {
            dest_bb->instructions.insert(it, range.begin(), range.end());
        } else {
            dest_bb->instructions.insert(dest_bb->instructions.end(), range.begin(), range.end());
        }
    }
    dest_bb->instr_count = dest_bb->instructions.size();
}

inline void MachineFunction::recompute_dominators() const {
    if (!dom_tree_cache_ptr_) {
        dom_tree_cache_ptr_ = new MachineDominatorTree();
    }
    static_cast<MachineDominatorTree*>(dom_tree_cache_ptr_)->compute(*this);
    dom_tree_valid_ = true;
}

inline const MachineDominatorTree& MachineFunction::dominators() const {
    if (!dom_tree_valid_) {
        recompute_dominators();
    }
    return *static_cast<const MachineDominatorTree*>(dom_tree_cache_ptr_);
}

inline bool MachineFunction::dominates(BlockId a, BlockId b) const {
    return dominators().dominates(a, b);
}

inline bool MachineFunction::strictly_dominates(BlockId a, BlockId b) const {
    return dominators().strictly_dominates(a, b);
}

inline bool MachineFunction::instruction_dominates(InstrId a, InstrId b) const {
    if (a == b) return true;
    const auto* ia = instruction(a);
    const auto* ib = instruction(b);
    if (!ia || !ib) return false;
    if (ia->parent_block == INVALID_BLOCK_ID || ib->parent_block == INVALID_BLOCK_ID) return false;

    if (ia->parent_block == ib->parent_block) {
        InstrId curr = ia->next_instr;
        while (curr != INVALID_INSTR_ID) {
            if (curr == b) return true;
            const auto* cinst = instruction(curr);
            if (!cinst) break;
            curr = cinst->next_instr;
        }
        return false;
    }

    return dominates(ia->parent_block, ib->parent_block);
}

inline bool MachineFunction::dominates(const MachineInstruction& a, const MachineInstruction& b) const {
    return instruction_dominates(a.id, b.id);
}

inline BlockId MachineFunction::nearest_common_dominator(BlockId a, BlockId b) const {
    return dominators().nearest_common_dominator(a, b);
}

inline void MachineFunction::recompute_dominance_frontier() const {
    if (!dom_frontier_cache_ptr_) {
        dom_frontier_cache_ptr_ = new MachineDominanceFrontier();
    }
    static_cast<MachineDominanceFrontier*>(dom_frontier_cache_ptr_)->compute(*this, dominators());
    dom_frontier_valid_ = true;
}

inline const MachineDominanceFrontier& MachineFunction::dominance_frontier() const {
    if (!dom_frontier_valid_) {
        recompute_dominance_frontier();
    }
    return *static_cast<const MachineDominanceFrontier*>(dom_frontier_cache_ptr_);
}

inline void MachineFunction::recompute_loop_info() const {
    if (!loop_info_cache_ptr_) {
        loop_info_cache_ptr_ = new MachineLoopInfo();
    }
    static_cast<MachineLoopInfo*>(loop_info_cache_ptr_)->compute(*this, dominators());
    loop_info_valid_ = true;
}

inline MachineLoopInfo& MachineFunction::loop_info() noexcept {
    if (!loop_info_valid_) {
        recompute_loop_info();
    }
    return *static_cast<MachineLoopInfo*>(loop_info_cache_ptr_);
}

inline const MachineLoopInfo& MachineFunction::loop_info() const noexcept {
    if (!loop_info_valid_) {
        recompute_loop_info();
    }
    return *static_cast<const MachineLoopInfo*>(loop_info_cache_ptr_);
}

inline std::vector<BlockId> MachineFunction::traversal_preorder() const {
    std::vector<BlockId> order;
    const BlockId root = entry_block();
    if (root == INVALID_BLOCK_ID || root >= blocks().size()) return order;
    std::vector<bool> visited(blocks().size(), false);
    std::vector<BlockId> stack;
    stack.push_back(root);
    visited[root] = true;

    while (!stack.empty()) {
        BlockId curr = stack.back();
        stack.pop_back();
        order.push_back(curr);
        const auto* bb = block(curr);
        if (!bb) continue;
        for (auto it = bb->successors.rbegin(); it != bb->successors.rend(); ++it) {
            BlockId succ = *it;
            if (succ < blocks().size() && !visited[succ]) {
                visited[succ] = true;
                stack.push_back(succ);
            }
        }
    }
    return order;
}

inline std::vector<BlockId> MachineFunction::traversal_postorder() const {
    std::vector<BlockId> order;
    const BlockId root = entry_block();
    if (root == INVALID_BLOCK_ID || root >= blocks().size()) return order;
    std::vector<bool> visited(blocks().size(), false);
    std::vector<std::pair<BlockId, std::size_t>> stack;
    visited[root] = true;
    stack.push_back({root, 0});

    while (!stack.empty()) {
        auto& [u, succ_idx] = stack.back();
        const auto* bb = block(u);
        if (bb && succ_idx < bb->successors.size()) {
            BlockId succ = bb->successors[succ_idx++];
            if (succ < blocks().size() && !visited[succ]) {
                visited[succ] = true;
                stack.push_back({succ, 0});
            }
        } else {
            order.push_back(u);
            stack.pop_back();
        }
    }
    return order;
}

inline std::vector<BlockId> MachineFunction::traversal_reverse_postorder() const {
    auto order = traversal_postorder();
    std::reverse(order.begin(), order.end());
    return order;
}

inline std::vector<BlockId> MachineFunction::traversal_breadth_first() const {
    std::vector<BlockId> order;
    const BlockId root = entry_block();
    if (root == INVALID_BLOCK_ID || root >= blocks().size()) return order;
    std::vector<bool> visited(blocks().size(), false);
    std::queue<BlockId> q;
    visited[root] = true;
    q.push(root);

    while (!q.empty()) {
        BlockId curr = q.front();
        q.pop();
        order.push_back(curr);
        const auto* bb = block(curr);
        if (!bb) continue;
        for (BlockId succ : bb->successors) {
            if (succ < blocks().size() && !visited[succ]) {
                visited[succ] = true;
                q.push(succ);
            }
        }
    }
    return order;
}

} // namespace dgtl::machine

module;

#include <algorithm>
#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <limits>
#include <optional>
#include <span>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <string>
#include <string_view>
#include <utility>

export module dgtl.mir.machine_ir:function;

import dgtl.lut_fsm;
import :types;
import :target;

export namespace dgtl::machine {

class MachineDominatorTree;
class MachineDominanceFrontier;
class MachineLoopInfo;

struct MachineBasicBlock {
    BlockId id = INVALID_BLOCK_ID;

    InstrId head_instr = INVALID_INSTR_ID;
    InstrId tail_instr = INVALID_INSTR_ID;
    std::size_t instr_count = 0;

    std::vector<InstrId> instructions;
    std::vector<BlockId> predecessors;
    std::vector<BlockId> successors;

    std::vector<MachineRegister> live_ins;
    std::vector<MachineRegister> live_outs;

    bool entry = false;
    bool exit = false;
    bool cold = false;
    bool address_taken = false;

    [[nodiscard]] bool valid() const noexcept {
        return id != INVALID_BLOCK_ID;
    }

    void add_instruction(InstrId instr) {
        instructions.push_back(instr);
    }

    void add_successor(BlockId b) {
        if (std::find(successors.begin(), successors.end(), b) == successors.end()) {
            successors.push_back(b);
        }
    }

    void remove_successor(BlockId b) {
        auto it = std::find(successors.begin(), successors.end(), b);
        if (it != successors.end()) {
            successors.erase(it);
        }
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool has_successor(BlockId b) const noexcept {
        return std::find(successors.begin(), successors.end(), b) != successors.end();
    }

    void add_predecessor(BlockId b) {
        if (std::find(predecessors.begin(), predecessors.end(), b) == predecessors.end()) {
            predecessors.push_back(b);
        }
    }

    void remove_predecessor(BlockId b) {
        auto it = std::find(predecessors.begin(), predecessors.end(), b);
        if (it != predecessors.end()) {
            predecessors.erase(it);
        }
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool has_predecessor(BlockId b) const noexcept {
        return std::find(predecessors.begin(), predecessors.end(), b) != predecessors.end();
    }
};

enum class StackSlotKind : std::uint8_t {
    Spill,
    Local,
    IncomingArgument,
    OutgoingArgument,
    CalleeSaved,
    VariableSized,
    Alignment,
    Temporary
};

struct MachineStackSlot {
    StackSlotId id = INVALID_STACK_SLOT;
    StackSlotKind kind = StackSlotKind::Temporary;

    std::uint32_t size_bytes = 0;
    std::uint32_t alignment = 1;

    std::int64_t offset = 0;
    bool fixed_offset = false;
    bool is_dead = false;
};

struct MachineFrameInfo {
    std::vector<MachineStackSlot> slots;

    std::uint32_t stack_alignment = 1;
    std::uint32_t outgoing_argument_bytes = 0;
    std::uint32_t local_bytes = 0;
    std::uint32_t spill_bytes = 0;
    std::uint32_t callee_saved_bytes = 0;
    std::vector<MachineRegister> callee_saved_registers;
    std::uint32_t alignment_padding = 0;
    std::uint32_t total_frame_bytes = 0;

    bool has_calls = false;
    bool has_variable_sized_objects = false;
    bool has_stack_protector = false;

    [[nodiscard]] StackSlotId create_slot(StackSlotKind kind,
                                          std::uint32_t size,
                                          std::uint32_t alignment) {
        const StackSlotId id = static_cast<StackSlotId>(slots.size());
        slots.push_back({
            id, kind, size, std::max<std::uint32_t>(alignment, 1u),
            0, false, false
        });
        return id;
    }
};

struct MachineProfile {
    std::uint32_t spill_count = 0;
    std::uint32_t reload_count = 0;
    std::uint32_t peak_register_pressure = 0;
    std::uint32_t estimated_cycle_cost = 0;
    std::uint32_t vector_lane_utilization = 0;
    std::uint32_t branch_mispredict_weight = 0;
    bool contains_atomic_fences = false;
    bool contains_accelerator_calls = false;
};

struct MachineConstantPoolEntry {
    std::uint32_t id = 0;
    std::vector<std::uint8_t> data;
    std::uint32_t alignment = 4;
    std::uint32_t offset = 0;
    bool is_machine_specific = false;
};

class MachineConstantPool {
    std::vector<MachineConstantPoolEntry> entries_;
    std::uint32_t total_size_ = 0;
    std::uint32_t max_alignment_ = 1;

public:
    [[nodiscard]] std::uint32_t add_entry(std::span<const std::uint8_t> bytes, std::uint32_t alignment = 4) {
        for (const auto& entry : entries_) {
            if (entry.alignment == alignment && entry.data.size() == bytes.size()) {
                if (std::equal(entry.data.begin(), entry.data.end(), bytes.begin())) {
                    return entry.id;
                }
            }
        }
        const std::uint32_t id = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(entries_.size());
        MachineConstantPoolEntry entry{};
        entry.id = id;
        entry.data.assign(bytes.begin(), bytes.end());
        entry.alignment = std::max(alignment, 1u);
        max_alignment_ = std::max(max_alignment_, entry.alignment);
        
        const std::uint32_t rem = total_size_ % entry.alignment;
        if (rem != 0) {
            total_size_ += (entry.alignment - rem);
        }
        entry.offset = total_size_;
        total_size_ += static_cast<std::uint32_t>(entry.data.size());

        entries_.push_back(std::move(entry));
        return id;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] std::uint32_t add_float(float val) {
        const auto* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const std::uint8_t*>(&val);
        return add_entry(std::span<const std::uint8_t>(bytes, sizeof(float)), alignof(float));
    }

    [[nodiscard]] std::uint32_t add_double(double val) {
        const auto* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const std::uint8_t*>(&val);
        return add_entry(std::span<const std::uint8_t>(bytes, sizeof(double)), alignof(double));
    }

    [[nodiscard]] std::uint32_t add_uint64(std::uint64_t val) {
        const auto* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const std::uint8_t*>(&val);
        return add_entry(std::span<const std::uint8_t>(bytes, sizeof(std::uint64_t)), alignof(std::uint64_t));
    }

    [[nodiscard]] const std::vector<MachineConstantPoolEntry>& entries() const noexcept {
        return entries_;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] const MachineConstantPoolEntry* entry(std::uint32_t id) const noexcept {
        if (id >= entries_.size()) return nullptr;
        return &entries_[id];
    }

    [[nodiscard]] std::uint32_t total_size() const noexcept { return total_size_; }
    [[nodiscard]] std::uint32_t max_alignment() const noexcept { return max_alignment_; }
    [[nodiscard]] bool empty() const noexcept { return entries_.empty(); }
    void clear() noexcept { entries_.clear(); total_size_ = 0; max_alignment_ = 1; }
};

struct MachineJumpTableEntry {
    std::uint32_t id = 0;
    std::vector<BlockId> targets;
    std::uint32_t alignment = 8;
    std::uint32_t offset = 0;
};

class MachineJumpTableInfo {
    std::vector<MachineJumpTableEntry> entries_;

public:
    [[nodiscard]] std::uint32_t create_jump_table(std::vector<BlockId> targets, std::uint32_t alignment = 8) {
        const std::uint32_t id = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(entries_.size());
        MachineJumpTableEntry entry{};
        entry.id = id;
        entry.targets = std::move(targets);
        entry.alignment = std::max(alignment, 1u);
        entries_.push_back(std::move(entry));
        return id;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] const std::vector<MachineJumpTableEntry>& entries() const noexcept {
        return entries_;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] const MachineJumpTableEntry* entry(std::uint32_t id) const noexcept {
        if (id >= entries_.size()) return nullptr;
        return &entries_[id];
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool empty() const noexcept { return entries_.empty(); }
    void clear() noexcept { entries_.clear(); }
};

enum class BlockBranchTopology : std::uint8_t {
    EmptyOrFallthrough = 0,
    UnconditionalBranch = 1,
    ConditionalBranchWithFallthrough = 2,
    ConditionalAndUnconditionalBranch = 3,
    Return = 4,
    SyscallOrTrap = 5,
    IndirectBranch = 6,
    Malformed = 7
};

struct MachineFunctionProperties {
    bool is_ssa = true;
    bool tracks_liveness = false;
    bool registers_allocated = false;
    bool frame_lowered = false;
    bool abi_lowered = false;
    bool contains_calls = false;
    bool contains_dynamic_alloca = false;
};

[[nodiscard]] constexpr dgtl::utils::BitMatrixLUT<8, 8> build_phase_transition_lut() noexcept {
    dgtl::utils::BitMatrixLUT<8, 8> lut{};
    for (std::size_t from = 0; from < 7; ++from) {
        for (std::size_t to = from + 1; to < 7; ++to) {
            lut.set(from, to, true);
        }
    }
    return lut;
}

inline constexpr auto kPhaseTransitionMatrix = build_phase_transition_lut();

class MachineFunction {
public:
    explicit MachineFunction(std::string_view name = {})
        : name_(name) {}

    [[nodiscard]] std::string_view name() const noexcept {
        return name_;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] MachinePhase phase() const noexcept {
        return phase_;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] static constexpr bool is_legal_phase_transition(MachinePhase from, MachinePhase to) noexcept {
        const auto f = static_cast<std::size_t>(from);
        const auto t = static_cast<std::size_t>(to);
        if (f >= 8 || t >= 8) return false;
        return kPhaseTransitionMatrix.get(f, t);
    }

    void set_phase(MachinePhase phase) noexcept {
        if (static_cast<std::uint8_t>(phase) >= static_cast<std::uint8_t>(phase_)) {
            phase_ = phase;
        }
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool advance_phase(MachinePhase expected_current,
                                     MachinePhase next) noexcept {
        if (phase_ != expected_current) {
            return false;
        }
        if (!is_legal_phase_transition(expected_current, next)) {
            return false;
        }
        phase_ = next;
        return true;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] MachineFunctionProperties& properties() noexcept {
        return properties_;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] const MachineFunctionProperties&
    properties() const noexcept {
        return properties_;
    }

    void invalidate_analysis_caches() noexcept;

    MachineBasicBlock& create_block(bool entry = false) {
        invalidate_analysis_caches();
        if (blocks_.size() >= static_cast<std::size_t>(INVALID_BLOCK_ID)) {
            throw std::overflow_error("MachineFunction block id exhausted");
        }
        const BlockId id = static_cast<BlockId>(blocks_.size());
        blocks_.push_back({});
        blocks_.back().id = id;
        blocks_.back().entry = entry;
        if (entry) {
            if (entry_block_ != INVALID_BLOCK_ID) {
                throw std::logic_error("MachineFunction cannot have multiple entry blocks");
            }
            entry_block_ = id;
        }
        return blocks_.back();
    }

    MachineBasicBlock* block(BlockId id) noexcept {
        if (id >= blocks_.size()) return nullptr;
        return &blocks_[id];
    }

    const MachineBasicBlock* block(BlockId id) const noexcept {
        if (id >= blocks_.size()) return nullptr;
        return &blocks_[id];
    }

    bool connect_blocks(BlockId from, BlockId to) {
        MachineBasicBlock* source = block(from);
        MachineBasicBlock* target = block(to);
        if (source == nullptr || target == nullptr) {
            return false;
        }
        invalidate_analysis_caches();
        source->add_successor(to);
        target->add_predecessor(from);
        return true;
    }

    bool disconnect_blocks(BlockId from, BlockId to) {
        MachineBasicBlock* source = block(from);
        MachineBasicBlock* target = block(to);
        if (source == nullptr || target == nullptr) {
            return false;
        }
        invalidate_analysis_caches();
        source->remove_successor(to);
        target->remove_predecessor(from);
        return true;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] std::span<MachineBasicBlock> blocks() noexcept {
        return blocks_;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] std::span<const MachineBasicBlock> blocks() const noexcept {
        return blocks_;
    }

    MachineInstruction& create_instruction() {
        return create_instruction(INVALID_MACHINE_OPCODE);
    }

    MachineInstruction& create_instruction(MachineOpcode opcode) {
        if (instructions_.size() >= static_cast<std::size_t>(INVALID_INSTR_ID)) {
            throw std::overflow_error("MachineFunction instruction id exhausted");
        }
        const InstrId id = static_cast<InstrId>(instructions_.size());
        instructions_.push_back({});
        instructions_.back().id = id;
        instructions_.back().opcode = opcode;
        return instructions_.back();
    }

    void insert_instruction_before(BlockId block_id, InstrId cursor_id, InstrId new_instr_id);
    void remove_instruction(InstrId instr_id);
    void splice_instructions(BlockId dest_block_id, InstrId cursor_id,
                             BlockId src_block_id, InstrId first_instr_id, InstrId last_instr_id);

    MachineInstruction* instruction(InstrId id) noexcept {
        if (id >= instructions_.size()) return nullptr;
        return &instructions_[id];
    }

    const MachineInstruction* instruction(InstrId id) const noexcept {
        if (id >= instructions_.size()) return nullptr;
        return &instructions_[id];
    }

    [[nodiscard]] std::span<MachineInstruction> instructions() noexcept {
        return instructions_;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] std::span<const MachineInstruction> instructions() const noexcept {
        return instructions_;
    }

    MachineRegister create_virtual_register(
        RegisterClassKind reg_class = RegisterClassKind::General) {
        if (next_virtual_reg_ == INVALID_REG) {
            throw std::overflow_error("MachineFunction virtual register id exhausted");
        }
        const RegId id = next_virtual_reg_++;
        return MachineRegister{id, RegisterKind::Virtual, reg_class};
    }

    void add_stack_slot(MachineStackSlot slot) {
        if (slot.id == INVALID_STACK_SLOT) {
            slot.id = static_cast<StackSlotId>(frame_.slots.size());
        }
        frame_.slots.push_back(slot);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] MachineFrameInfo& frame_info() noexcept {
        return frame_;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] const MachineFrameInfo& frame_info() const noexcept {
        return frame_;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] BlockId entry_block() const noexcept {
        return entry_block_;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] std::size_t virtual_register_count() const noexcept {
        return next_virtual_reg_;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool assign_target_opcode(
        InstrId instruction_id, MachineOpcode target_opcode) noexcept {
        MachineInstruction* inst = instruction(instruction_id);
        return inst != nullptr && inst->assign_target_opcode_once(target_opcode);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool has_target_opcodes() const noexcept {
        for (const MachineInstruction& inst : instructions_) {
            if (!inst.has_target_opcode()) {
                return false;
            }
        }
        return true;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool is_phase_at_least(MachinePhase phase) const noexcept {
        return static_cast<std::uint8_t>(phase_) >= static_cast<std::uint8_t>(phase);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool has_virtual_registers() const noexcept {
        for (const MachineInstruction& inst : instructions_) {
            for (const MachineOperand& op : inst.operands) {
                if (op.kind == MachineOperandKind::Register && op.reg.is_virtual()) {
                    return true;
                }
                if (op.kind == MachineOperandKind::Memory) {
                    if (op.memory.base.is_virtual() || op.memory.index.is_virtual()) {
                        return true;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool replace_virtual_register(
        MachineRegister virtual_reg,
        MachineRegister physical_reg) noexcept {
        if (!virtual_reg.is_virtual() || !physical_reg.is_physical()) {
            return false;
        }

        bool replaced = false;
        for (MachineInstruction& inst : instructions_) {
            for (MachineOperand& op : inst.operands) {
                if (op.kind == MachineOperandKind::Register && op.reg == virtual_reg) {
                    op.reg = physical_reg;
                    replaced = true;
                }

                if (op.kind == MachineOperandKind::Memory) {
                    if (op.memory.base == virtual_reg) {
                        op.memory.base = physical_reg;
                        replaced = true;
                    }
                    if (op.memory.index == virtual_reg) {
                        op.memory.index = physical_reg;
                        replaced = true;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        return replaced;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool mark_target_lowered() noexcept {
        if (!has_target_opcodes()) {
            return false;
        }
        if (static_cast<std::uint8_t>(phase_) < static_cast<std::uint8_t>(MachinePhase::PreRegAlloc)) {
            phase_ = MachinePhase::PreRegAlloc;
        }
        return true;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool mark_frame_lowered() noexcept {
        if (!properties_.registers_allocated || has_virtual_registers()) {
            return false;
        }
        properties_.frame_lowered = true;
        phase_ = MachinePhase::FrameLowered;
        return true;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool mark_abi_lowered() noexcept {
        if (!properties_.frame_lowered) {
            return false;
        }
        properties_.abi_lowered = true;
        phase_ = MachinePhase::ABILowered;
        return true;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool mark_emission_ready() noexcept {
        if (!properties_.abi_lowered || has_virtual_registers() || !has_target_opcodes()) {
            return false;
        }
        phase_ = MachinePhase::EmissionReady;
        return true;
    }

    void mark_registers_allocated() noexcept {
        properties_.registers_allocated = has_target_opcodes() && !has_virtual_registers();
        if (properties_.registers_allocated && is_phase_at_least(MachinePhase::PreRegAlloc)) {
            phase_ = MachinePhase::PostRegAlloc;
        }
    }

    [[nodiscard]] MachineConstantPool& constant_pool() noexcept {
        return constant_pool_;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] const MachineConstantPool& constant_pool() const noexcept {
        return constant_pool_;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] MachineJumpTableInfo& jump_table_info() noexcept {
        return jump_table_info_;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] const MachineJumpTableInfo& jump_table_info() const noexcept {
        return jump_table_info_;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] MachineProfile& profile() noexcept {
        return profile_;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] const MachineProfile& profile() const noexcept {
        return profile_;
    }

    void recompute_dominators() const;
    [[nodiscard]] const MachineDominatorTree& dominators() const;
    [[nodiscard]] bool dominates(BlockId a, BlockId b) const;
    [[nodiscard]] bool strictly_dominates(BlockId a, BlockId b) const;
    [[nodiscard]] bool instruction_dominates(InstrId a, InstrId b) const;
    [[nodiscard]] bool dominates(const MachineInstruction& a, const MachineInstruction& b) const;
    [[nodiscard]] BlockId nearest_common_dominator(BlockId a, BlockId b) const;

    void recompute_dominance_frontier() const;
    [[nodiscard]] const MachineDominanceFrontier& dominance_frontier() const;

    void recompute_loop_info() const;
    [[nodiscard]] MachineLoopInfo& loop_info() noexcept;
    [[nodiscard]] const MachineLoopInfo& loop_info() const noexcept;

    [[nodiscard]] std::vector<BlockId> traversal_preorder() const;
    [[nodiscard]] std::vector<BlockId> traversal_postorder() const;
    [[nodiscard]] std::vector<BlockId> traversal_reverse_postorder() const;
    [[nodiscard]] std::vector<BlockId> traversal_breadth_first() const;

    [[nodiscard]] bool verify_cfg_integrity(std::string* out_error = nullptr) const;
    [[nodiscard]] bool verify_ssa_dominance(std::string* out_error = nullptr) const;
    [[nodiscard]] BlockBranchTopology block_branch_topology(BlockId block_id) const noexcept;

private:
    std::string name_;
    MachinePhase phase_ = MachinePhase::Selected;
    MachineFunctionProperties properties_{};

    std::vector<MachineBasicBlock> blocks_;
    std::vector<MachineInstruction> instructions_;

    MachineFrameInfo frame_{};
    MachineConstantPool constant_pool_{};
    MachineJumpTableInfo jump_table_info_{};
    MachineProfile profile_{};

    mutable bool dom_tree_valid_ = false;
    mutable void* dom_tree_cache_ptr_ = nullptr;
    mutable bool dom_frontier_valid_ = false;
    mutable void* dom_frontier_cache_ptr_ = nullptr;
    mutable bool loop_info_valid_ = false;
    mutable void* loop_info_cache_ptr_ = nullptr;

    RegId next_virtual_reg_ = 0;
    BlockId entry_block_ = INVALID_BLOCK_ID;
};

/* ============================================================================
 * MachineSelection Bridge & Builder
 * ========================================================================== */

enum class MachineSelectionOperandKind : std::uint8_t {
    Invalid,
    Value,
    Immediate,
    FloatingImmediate,
    Memory,
    BasicBlock,
    Symbol,
    StackSlot,
    Register
};

struct MachineSelectionOperand {
    MachineSelectionOperandKind kind = MachineSelectionOperandKind::Invalid;
    std::uint32_t value = INVALID_MACHINE_ID;
    std::int64_t immediate = 0;
    double floating_immediate = 0.0;
    MachineMemoryOperand memory = {};
    BlockId block = INVALID_BLOCK_ID;
    SymbolId symbol = INVALID_SYMBOL;
    StackSlotId stack_slot = INVALID_STACK_SLOT;
    MachineRegister reg = {};
    RegisterClassKind reg_class = RegisterClassKind::Invalid;
    OperandRole role = OperandRole::Use;

    [[nodiscard]] static MachineSelectionOperand value_ref(
        std::uint32_t id,
        OperandRole role = OperandRole::Use) noexcept {
        MachineSelectionOperand operand;
        operand.kind = MachineSelectionOperandKind::Value;
        operand.value = id;
        operand.role = role;
        return operand;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] static MachineSelectionOperand imm(
        std::int64_t v) noexcept {
        MachineSelectionOperand operand;
        operand.kind = MachineSelectionOperandKind::Immediate;
        operand.immediate = v;
        return operand;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] static MachineSelectionOperand block_ref(
        BlockId id) noexcept {
        MachineSelectionOperand operand;
        operand.kind = MachineSelectionOperandKind::BasicBlock;
        operand.block = id;
        return operand;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] static MachineSelectionOperand memory_ref(
        const MachineMemoryOperand& mem,
        OperandRole role = OperandRole::Use) noexcept {
        MachineSelectionOperand operand;
        operand.kind = MachineSelectionOperandKind::Memory;
        operand.memory = mem;
        operand.role = role;
        return operand;
    }
};

struct MachineSelection {
    MachineForm form = MachineForm::Unhandled;
    MachineSemanticKind semantic_kind = MachineSemanticKind::None;
    MachineOpcode opcode = GM_INVALID;
    std::uint32_t semantic_provenance = 0;
    std::uint32_t debug_id = 0;
    std::uint32_t result_value = INVALID_MACHINE_ID;
    RegisterClassKind result_class = RegisterClassKind::General;

    std::vector<MachineSelectionOperand> operands;

    MachineInstrFlag flags = MachineInstrFlag::None;

    MachineTrait traits = MachineTrait::None;
    MachineDomain domains = MachineDomain::None;
    MachineAttribute attributes = MachineAttribute::None;
    std::uint64_t intent_hash = 0ULL;
    std::uint32_t epoch = 0;
    std::uint16_t latency_cycles = 1;
    std::uint16_t throughput_reciprocal = 1;
};

class MachineFunctionBuilder {
public:
    explicit MachineFunctionBuilder(MachineFunction& function)
        : function_(function) {}

    [[nodiscard]] MachineRegister value_register(
        std::uint32_t value_id,
        RegisterClassKind reg_class = RegisterClassKind::General) {
        if (value_id == INVALID_MACHINE_ID) {
            return {};
        }
        if (value_id >= value_registers_.size()) {
            value_registers_.resize(static_cast<std::size_t>(value_id) + 1u);
        }
        MachineRegister& cached = value_registers_[value_id];
        if (!cached.valid()) {
            cached = function_.create_virtual_register(reg_class);
        }
        return cached;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] MachineInstruction& append(
        BlockId block,
        const MachineSelection& selection,
        RegisterClassKind result_class = RegisterClassKind::General) {
        MachineBasicBlock* bb = function_.block(block);
        if (bb == nullptr) {
            throw std::out_of_range("invalid MachineFunction block");
        }

        if (selection.form == MachineForm::Unhandled) {
            throw std::invalid_argument("cannot append unhandled MachineForm");
        }

        MachineInstruction& instruction =
            function_.create_instruction();
        instruction.form = selection.form;
        instruction.semantic_kind = selection.semantic_kind;
        instruction.opcode = selection.opcode != GM_INVALID ? selection.opcode : GM_NOOP;
        instruction.semantic_provenance = selection.semantic_provenance;
        instruction.debug_id = selection.debug_id;
        instruction.flags = selection.flags;
        instruction.traits = selection.traits;
        instruction.domains = selection.domains;
        instruction.attributes = selection.attributes;
        instruction.intent_hash = selection.intent_hash;
        instruction.epoch = selection.epoch;
        instruction.latency_cycles = selection.latency_cycles;
        instruction.throughput_reciprocal = selection.throughput_reciprocal;
        instruction.parent_block = block;

        const RegisterClassKind effective_result_class =
            selection.result_class == RegisterClassKind::Invalid
                ? result_class
                : selection.result_class;

        if (selection.result_value != INVALID_MACHINE_ID) {
            instruction.add_operand(
                MachineOperand::def(
                    value_register(selection.result_value, effective_result_class)));
        }

        if (!selection.operands.empty()) {
            for (const MachineSelectionOperand& source : selection.operands) {
                switch (source.kind) {
                case MachineSelectionOperandKind::Value:
                    if (source.value != INVALID_MACHINE_ID) {
                        const RegisterClassKind operand_class =
                            source.reg_class == RegisterClassKind::Invalid
                                ? RegisterClassKind::General
                                : source.reg_class;
                        MachineRegister value_reg =
                            value_register(source.value, operand_class);
                        if (source.role == OperandRole::Def) {
                            instruction.add_operand(MachineOperand::def(value_reg));
                        } else {
                            MachineOperand operand = MachineOperand::use(value_reg);
                            operand.role = source.role;
                            instruction.add_operand(std::move(operand));
                        }
                    }
                    break;
                case MachineSelectionOperandKind::Immediate:
                    instruction.add_operand(MachineOperand::imm(source.immediate));
                    break;
                case MachineSelectionOperandKind::FloatingImmediate: {
                    MachineOperand operand;
                    operand.kind = MachineOperandKind::FloatingImmediate;
                    operand.role = source.role;
                    operand.floating_immediate = source.floating_immediate;
                    instruction.add_operand(std::move(operand));
                    break;
                }
                case MachineSelectionOperandKind::Memory: {
                    MachineOperand operand;
                    operand.kind = MachineOperandKind::Memory;
                    operand.role = source.role;
                    operand.memory = source.memory;
                    if (operand.memory.base.valid() && operand.memory.base.is_virtual()) {
                        operand.memory.base = value_register(operand.memory.base.id, RegisterClassKind::General);
                    }
                    if (operand.memory.index.valid() && operand.memory.index.is_virtual()) {
                        operand.memory.index = value_register(operand.memory.index.id, RegisterClassKind::General);
                    }
                    instruction.add_operand(std::move(operand));
                    break;
                }
                case MachineSelectionOperandKind::BasicBlock:
                    instruction.add_operand(MachineOperand::block_ref(source.block));
                    break;
                case MachineSelectionOperandKind::Symbol: {
                    MachineOperand operand;
                    operand.kind = MachineOperandKind::Symbol;
                    operand.role = source.role;
                    operand.symbol = source.symbol;
                    instruction.add_operand(std::move(operand));
                    break;
                }
                case MachineSelectionOperandKind::StackSlot:
                    instruction.add_operand(MachineOperand::stack_ref(source.stack_slot));
                    break;
                case MachineSelectionOperandKind::Register: {
                    MachineOperand operand;
                    operand.kind = MachineOperandKind::Register;
                    operand.role = source.role;
                    operand.reg = source.reg;
                    instruction.add_operand(std::move(operand));
                    break;
                }
                case MachineSelectionOperandKind::Invalid:
                    throw std::invalid_argument("invalid MachineSelection operand");
                }
            }
        }

        function_.insert_instruction_before(block, INVALID_INSTR_ID, instruction.id);

        if (instruction.has_flag(MachineInstrFlag::Call)) {
            function_.properties().contains_calls = true;
            function_.frame_info().has_calls = true;
        }
        return instruction;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] MachineBasicBlock& create_block(bool entry = false) {
        return function_.create_block(entry);
    }

private:
    MachineFunction& function_;
    std::vector<MachineRegister> value_registers_;
};

enum class EmissionKind : std::uint8_t {
    Assembly,
    Object,
    MachineCode,
    Intermediate
};

struct EmissionResult {
    bool success = false;
    std::vector<std::uint8_t> bytes;
    std::string_view diagnostic = {};
};

class MachineEmitter {
public:
    virtual ~MachineEmitter() = default;

    [[nodiscard]] virtual EmissionResult
    emit(const MachineFunction& function) = 0;

    [[nodiscard]] virtual EmissionKind kind() const noexcept = 0;
};

struct MIR {
    MachineFunction function;

    explicit MIR(std::string_view name = {})
        : function(name) {}

    [[nodiscard]] MachineFunction& machine_function() noexcept {
        return function;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] const MachineFunction&
    machine_function() const noexcept {
        return function;
    }
};

} // namespace dgtl::machine
