class MachineFunction;

class TargetRegisterInfo {
public:
    virtual ~TargetRegisterInfo() = default;

    [[nodiscard]] virtual std::size_t physical_register_count() const noexcept = 0;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual bool is_allocatable(MachineRegister reg) const noexcept = 0;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual RegisterClassKind register_class(MachineRegister reg) const noexcept = 0;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual bool aliases(MachineRegister lhs, MachineRegister rhs) const noexcept = 0;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual bool is_reserved(MachineRegister reg) const noexcept = 0;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual bool is_caller_saved(MachineRegister reg) const noexcept = 0;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual bool is_callee_saved(MachineRegister reg) const noexcept = 0;
};

struct MachineInstructionInfo {
    std::uint16_t required_operand_count = 0;
    std::uint16_t result_count = 0;
    std::uint32_t register_class_mask = 0;

    bool may_load = false;
    bool may_store = false;
    bool is_call = false;
    bool is_return = false;
    bool is_branch = false;
    bool is_terminator = false;
    bool is_pseudo = false;
    bool is_barrier = false;
    bool is_atomic = false;
    bool is_move = false;
    bool commutable = false;

    MachineTrait default_traits = MachineTrait::None;
    MachineDomain default_domains = MachineDomain::None;
    MachineAttribute default_attributes = MachineAttribute::None;
    std::uint16_t default_latency = 1;
};

enum class MachineRegisterConstraintKind : std::uint8_t {
    None,
    FixedRegister,
    RegisterClass
};

struct MachineRegisterConstraint {
    MachineRegisterConstraintKind kind = MachineRegisterConstraintKind::None;
    MachineRegister fixed_register = {};
    RegisterClassKind required_class = RegisterClassKind::Invalid;

    [[nodiscard]] static constexpr MachineRegisterConstraint none() noexcept {
        return {};
    }

    [[nodiscard]] static constexpr MachineRegisterConstraint fixed(
        MachineRegister reg) noexcept {
        return {
            MachineRegisterConstraintKind::FixedRegister,
            reg,
            reg.reg_class
        };
    }

    [[nodiscard]] static constexpr MachineRegisterConstraint
    register_class(RegisterClassKind cls) noexcept {
        return {
            MachineRegisterConstraintKind::RegisterClass,
            {},
            cls
        };
    }
};

class TargetInstructionInfo {
public:
    virtual ~TargetInstructionInfo() = default;

    [[nodiscard]] virtual MachineOpcode
    opcode_for(MachineForm form,
               MachineSemanticKind semantic_kind) const noexcept {
        (void)form;
        (void)semantic_kind;
        return INVALID_MACHINE_OPCODE;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] virtual MachineInstructionInfo
    info(MachineOpcode opcode) const noexcept = 0;

    [[nodiscard]] virtual bool
    is_legal(MachineOpcode opcode) const noexcept = 0;

    [[nodiscard]] virtual bool
    is_copy(MachineOpcode opcode) const noexcept = 0;

    [[nodiscard]] virtual bool
    is_move(MachineOpcode opcode) const noexcept = 0;

    [[nodiscard]] virtual MachineRegisterConstraint
    register_constraint(MachineOpcode opcode,
                        std::size_t operand_index) const noexcept {
        (void)opcode;
        (void)operand_index;
        return MachineRegisterConstraint::none();
    }

    [[nodiscard]] virtual MachineOpcode
    load_opcode(RegisterClassKind reg_class) const noexcept {
        (void)reg_class;
        return INVALID_MACHINE_OPCODE;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] virtual MachineOpcode
    store_opcode(RegisterClassKind reg_class) const noexcept {
        (void)reg_class;
        return INVALID_MACHINE_OPCODE;
    }
};

class CommonTargetInstructionInfo : public TargetInstructionInfo {
public:
    [[nodiscard]] static constexpr MachineInstructionInfo default_form_info(MachineForm form) noexcept {
        MachineInstructionInfo result{};
        switch (form) {
            case MachineForm::LoadStack:
                result.may_load = true;
                result.required_operand_count = 2;
                result.result_count = 1;
                result.default_domains = MachineDomain::Memory;
                result.default_traits = MachineTrait::IsMemoryRead | MachineTrait::IsMemoryOp;
                result.default_latency = 3;
                break;
            case MachineForm::StoreStack:
                result.may_store = true;
                result.required_operand_count = 2;
                result.default_domains = MachineDomain::Memory;
                result.default_traits = MachineTrait::IsMemoryWrite | MachineTrait::IsMemoryOp;
                result.default_latency = 1;
                break;
            case MachineForm::Call:
                result.is_call = true;
                result.required_operand_count = 1;
                result.default_domains = MachineDomain::ControlFlow;
                result.default_latency = 10;
                break;
            case MachineForm::Return:
                result.is_return = true;
                result.is_terminator = true;
                result.default_domains = MachineDomain::ControlFlow | MachineDomain::Terminator;
                result.default_traits = MachineTrait::IsTerminator;
                result.default_latency = 1;
                break;
            case MachineForm::Goto:
            case MachineForm::BranchCond:
            case MachineForm::BranchCompare:
                result.is_branch = true;
                result.is_terminator = true;
                result.default_domains = MachineDomain::ControlFlow | MachineDomain::Terminator;
                result.default_traits = MachineTrait::IsTerminator;
                result.default_latency = 1;
                break;
            case MachineForm::HardwareFence:
                result.is_barrier = true;
                result.default_domains = MachineDomain::Concurrency;
                result.default_traits = MachineTrait::MemoryBarrier;
                result.default_latency = 5;
                break;
            case MachineForm::AtomicOp:
                result.is_atomic = true;
                result.default_domains = MachineDomain::Concurrency | MachineDomain::Memory;
                result.default_traits = MachineTrait::IsMemoryOp;
                result.default_latency = 12;
                break;
            case MachineForm::Move:
                result.is_move = true;
                result.required_operand_count = 2;
                result.result_count = 1;
                result.default_domains = MachineDomain::Arithmetic;
                result.default_traits = MachineTrait::HasNoSideEffects;
                result.default_latency = 1;
                break;
            case MachineForm::LoadImmediate:
                result.required_operand_count = 2;
                result.result_count = 1;
                result.default_domains = MachineDomain::Arithmetic;
                result.default_traits = MachineTrait::ConstantFoldable | MachineTrait::HasNoSideEffects;
                result.default_latency = 1;
                break;
            case MachineForm::IntegerBinary32:
            case MachineForm::IntegerBinary64:
                result.required_operand_count = 3;
                result.result_count = 1;
                result.default_domains = MachineDomain::Arithmetic;
                result.default_traits = MachineTrait::HasNoSideEffects;
                result.default_latency = 1;
                break;
            case MachineForm::FloatBinary32:
            case MachineForm::FloatBinary64:
                result.required_operand_count = 3;
                result.result_count = 1;
                result.default_domains = MachineDomain::Arithmetic;
                result.default_traits = MachineTrait::HasNoSideEffects;
                result.default_latency = 3;
                break;
            case MachineForm::Compare32:
                result.required_operand_count = 3;
                result.result_count = 1;
                result.default_domains = MachineDomain::Comparison;
                result.default_traits = MachineTrait::HasNoSideEffects;
                result.default_latency = 1;
                break;
            case MachineForm::FloatTernaryFma32:
                result.required_operand_count = 4;
                result.result_count = 1;
                result.default_domains = MachineDomain::Arithmetic;
                result.default_traits = MachineTrait::IsFusible | MachineTrait::HasNoSideEffects;
                result.default_latency = 4;
                break;
            case MachineForm::VectorBinary512:
                result.required_operand_count = 3;
                result.result_count = 1;
                result.default_domains = MachineDomain::SIMD;
                result.default_traits = MachineTrait::IsVectorizable | MachineTrait::HasNoSideEffects;
                result.default_latency = 3;
                break;
            case MachineForm::VectorGather512:
                result.may_load = true;
                result.required_operand_count = 3;
                result.result_count = 1;
                result.default_domains = MachineDomain::SIMD | MachineDomain::Memory;
                result.default_traits = MachineTrait::IsMemoryRead | MachineTrait::IsVectorizable;
                result.default_latency = 8;
                break;
            case MachineForm::VectorStore512:
                result.may_store = true;
                result.required_operand_count = 3;
                result.default_domains = MachineDomain::SIMD | MachineDomain::Memory;
                result.default_traits = MachineTrait::IsMemoryWrite | MachineTrait::IsVectorizable;
                result.default_latency = 2;
                break;
            case MachineForm::Syscall:
                result.is_call = true;
                result.is_barrier = true;
                result.default_domains = MachineDomain::ControlFlow;
                result.default_traits = MachineTrait::MemoryBarrier;
                result.default_latency = 50;
                break;
            case MachineForm::TensorOp:
                result.required_operand_count = 3;
                result.result_count = 1;
                result.default_domains = MachineDomain::Tensor;
                result.default_traits = MachineTrait::IsTensor;
                result.default_latency = 16;
                break;
            case MachineForm::ConcurrentSync:
                result.is_barrier = true;
                result.default_domains = MachineDomain::Concurrency;
                result.default_traits = MachineTrait::MemoryBarrier;
                result.default_latency = 10;
                break;
            case MachineForm::NoOp:
            case MachineForm::Unhandled:
            default:
                break;
        }
        return result;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool is_legal(MachineOpcode opcode) const noexcept override {
        return opcode != INVALID_MACHINE_OPCODE;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool is_copy(MachineOpcode opcode) const noexcept override {
        return opcode == GM_COPY;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool is_move(MachineOpcode opcode) const noexcept override {
        return opcode == GM_COPY;
    }
};

class TargetFrameInfo {
public:
    virtual ~TargetFrameInfo() = default;

    [[nodiscard]] virtual std::uint32_t stack_alignment() const noexcept = 0;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual std::uint32_t spill_alignment(RegisterClassKind) const noexcept = 0;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual std::uint32_t spill_size(RegisterClassKind) const noexcept = 0;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual std::uint32_t outgoing_argument_alignment() const noexcept = 0;
};

struct MachineFrameLayout {
    std::uint32_t local_bytes = 0;
    std::uint32_t spill_bytes = 0;
    std::uint32_t callee_saved_bytes = 0;
    std::uint32_t outgoing_args_bytes = 0;
    std::uint32_t alignment_padding = 0;
    std::uint32_t total_frame_bytes = 0;
    std::vector<MachineRegister> saved_registers;
};

class CommonTargetFrameInfo : public TargetFrameInfo {
public:
    [[nodiscard]] std::uint32_t stack_alignment() const noexcept override {
        return 16;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] std::uint32_t spill_alignment(RegisterClassKind cls) const noexcept override {
        switch (cls) {
            case RegisterClassKind::Vector: return 16;
            case RegisterClassKind::FloatingPoint: return 8;
            default: return 8;
        }
    }

    [[nodiscard]] std::uint32_t spill_size(RegisterClassKind cls) const noexcept override {
        switch (cls) {
            case RegisterClassKind::Vector: return 16;
            case RegisterClassKind::FloatingPoint: return 8;
            default: return 8;
        }
    }

    [[nodiscard]] std::uint32_t outgoing_argument_alignment() const noexcept override {
        return 8;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] MachineFrameLayout compute_layout(
        std::uint32_t local_bytes,
        std::uint32_t spill_bytes,
        std::uint32_t outgoing_bytes,
        std::span<const MachineRegister> clobbered_registers) const noexcept {
        MachineFrameLayout layout{};
        layout.local_bytes = local_bytes;
        layout.spill_bytes = spill_bytes;
        layout.outgoing_args_bytes = outgoing_bytes;

        for (const auto& reg : clobbered_registers) {
            layout.saved_registers.push_back(reg);
        }
        layout.callee_saved_bytes = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(layout.saved_registers.size() * 8);

        const std::uint32_t raw_sum = layout.local_bytes + layout.spill_bytes +
                                      layout.callee_saved_bytes + layout.outgoing_args_bytes;
        const std::uint32_t align = stack_alignment();
        if (align > 1) {
            const std::uint32_t rem = raw_sum % align;
            if (rem != 0) {
                layout.alignment_padding = align - rem;
            }
        }
        layout.total_frame_bytes = raw_sum + layout.alignment_padding;
        return layout;
    }
};

/* ============================================================================
 * ABI contract
 * ========================================================================== */

enum class ABIValueKind : std::uint8_t {
    Integer,
    FloatingPoint,
    Vector,
    Aggregate,
    Pointer,
    Predicate,
    Memory
};

struct ABIValue {
    ABIValueKind kind = ABIValueKind::Integer;
    std::uint32_t size_bytes = 0;
    std::uint32_t alignment = 1;
    RegisterClassKind preferred_class = RegisterClassKind::Invalid;
};

enum class ABIArgLocationKind : std::uint8_t {
    Invalid,
    Register,
    Stack,
    Indirect
};

struct ABIArgLocation {
    ABIArgLocationKind kind = ABIArgLocationKind::Invalid;
    MachineRegister reg = {};
    std::int32_t stack_offset = 0;
    std::uint32_t size_bytes = 0;
};

struct ABICallPlan {
    std::vector<ABIArgLocation> arguments;
    ABIArgLocation return_value = {};
    std::uint32_t stack_bytes = 0;
    std::uint32_t stack_alignment = 1;
    std::uint32_t shadow_space_bytes = 0;
};

class TargetABI {
public:
    virtual ~TargetABI() = default;

    [[nodiscard]] virtual ABICallPlan
    classify_call(std::span<const ABIValue> arguments,
                  std::optional<ABIValue> return_value) const = 0;

    [[nodiscard]] virtual bool
    is_caller_saved(MachineRegister reg) const noexcept = 0;

    [[nodiscard]] virtual bool
    is_callee_saved(MachineRegister reg) const noexcept = 0;

    [[nodiscard]] virtual MachineRegister
    stack_pointer_register() const noexcept = 0;

    [[nodiscard]] virtual MachineRegister
    frame_pointer_register() const noexcept = 0;

    [[nodiscard]] virtual bool
    lower_function(MachineFunction& function) const {
        (void)function;
        return false;
    }
};

class MachineCodeEmitter {
public:
    virtual ~MachineCodeEmitter() = default;

    virtual void emit_instruction(const MachineInstruction& inst,
                                  std::vector<std::uint8_t>& out) = 0;
};

struct TargetSubtarget {
    const TargetRegisterInfo& reg_info;
    const TargetInstructionInfo& instr_info;
    const TargetFrameInfo& frame_info;
    const TargetABI& abi;
    const MachineCodeEmitter* emitter = nullptr;
};

} // namespace dgtl::machine

/* ============================================================================
 * Core identifiers
 * ========================================================================== */

using MachineId   = std::uint32_t;
using BlockId     = std::uint32_t;
using InstrId     = std::uint32_t;
using RegId       = std::uint32_t;
using StackSlotId = std::uint32_t;
using SymbolId    = std::uint32_t;

inline constexpr MachineId INVALID_MACHINE_ID = 0xFFFFFFFFu;
inline constexpr BlockId INVALID_BLOCK_ID = 0xFFFFFFFFu;
inline constexpr InstrId INVALID_INSTR_ID = 0xFFFFFFFFu;
inline constexpr RegId INVALID_REG = 0xFFFFFFFFu;
inline constexpr StackSlotId INVALID_STACK_SLOT = 0xFFFFFFFFu;
inline constexpr SymbolId INVALID_SYMBOL = 0xFFFFFFFFu;

/* ============================================================================
 * Machine IR state
 * ========================================================================== */

enum class MachinePhase : std::uint8_t {
    Selected,
    Legalized,
    PreRegAlloc,
    PostRegAlloc,
    FrameLowered,
    ABILowered,
    EmissionReady
};

enum class RegisterKind : std::uint8_t {
    Virtual,
    Physical
};

enum class RegisterClassKind : std::uint8_t {
    Invalid,
    General,
    Integer,
    FloatingPoint,
    Vector,
    Predicate,
    Mask,
    Address,
    Special,
    Custom
};

/* ============================================================================
 * Register model
 * ========================================================================== */

struct MachineRegister {
    RegId id = INVALID_REG;
    RegisterKind kind = RegisterKind::Virtual;
    RegisterClassKind reg_class = RegisterClassKind::Invalid;

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool valid() const noexcept {
        return id != INVALID_REG;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_virtual() const noexcept {
        return kind == RegisterKind::Virtual;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_physical() const noexcept {
        return kind == RegisterKind::Physical;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] static constexpr MachineRegister virtual_reg(
        RegId reg_id,
        RegisterClassKind cls = RegisterClassKind::General) noexcept {
        MachineRegister r;
        r.id = reg_id;
        r.kind = RegisterKind::Virtual;
        r.reg_class = cls;
        return r;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] static constexpr MachineRegister physical_reg(
        RegId reg_id,
        RegisterClassKind cls = RegisterClassKind::General) noexcept {
        MachineRegister r;
        r.id = reg_id;
        r.kind = RegisterKind::Physical;
        r.reg_class = cls;
        return r;
    }

    friend constexpr bool operator==(const MachineRegister&,
                                     const MachineRegister&) noexcept = default;
};

struct RegisterMask {
    std::vector<std::uint64_t> words;

    void resize(std::size_t bit_count) {
        words.resize((bit_count + 63u) / 64u);
    }

    void clear() noexcept {
        std::fill(words.begin(), words.end(), 0u);
    }

    void set(std::size_t bit) {
        const std::size_t word = bit / 64u;
        if (word >= words.size()) {
            words.resize(word + 1u);
        }
        words[word] |= (std::uint64_t{1} << (bit % 64u));
    }

    void reset(std::size_t bit) noexcept {
        const std::size_t word = bit / 64u;
        if (word < words.size()) {
            words[word] &= ~(std::uint64_t{1} << (bit % 64u));
        }
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool test(std::size_t bit) const noexcept {
        const std::size_t word = bit / 64u;
        return word < words.size() &&
               (words[word] & (std::uint64_t{1} << (bit % 64u))) != 0;
    }
};

/* ============================================================================
 * Machine operands
 * ========================================================================== */

enum class MachineOperandKind : std::uint8_t {
    Invalid,
    Register,
    Immediate,
    FloatingImmediate,
    Memory,
    BasicBlock,
    Symbol,
    StackSlot,
    ConstantPoolIndex,
    JumpTableIndex,
    RegisterMask,
    Metadata
};

enum class OperandRole : std::uint8_t {
    Use,
    Def,
    UseDef,
    Kill,
    Dead,
    ImplicitUse,
    ImplicitDef,
    Debug
};

enum class RelocationKind : std::uint8_t {
    None,
    Absolute,
    PCRelative,
    GOT,
    PLT,
    TLS,
    TargetSpecific
};

struct MachineMemoryOperand {
    MachineRegister base = {};
    MachineRegister index = {};
    std::int32_t scale = 1;
    std::int64_t displacement = 0;
    std::uint32_t address_space = 0;
    std::uint32_t size_bytes = 0;
    std::uint32_t alignment = 1;

    bool load = false;
    bool store = false;
    bool volatile_access = false;
    bool atomic = false;
    bool invariant = false;
};

enum class SubRegIndex : std::uint8_t {
    None = 0,
    SubLow32 = 1,
    SubHigh32 = 2,
    SubLow16 = 3,
    SubHigh16 = 4,
    SubLow8 = 5,
    SubHigh8 = 6,
    SubLow64 = 7,
    SubHigh64 = 8,
    SubLow128 = 9,
    SubHigh128 = 10,
    SubLow256 = 11,
    SubHigh256 = 12
};

using LaneBitmask = std::uint64_t;
inline constexpr LaneBitmask kAllLanes = ~0ULL;

struct alignas(16) MachineOperand {
    MachineOperandKind kind = MachineOperandKind::Invalid;
    OperandRole role = OperandRole::Use;

    MachineRegister reg = {};
    std::int64_t immediate = 0;
    double floating_immediate = 0.0;

    MachineMemoryOperand memory = {};
    BlockId block = INVALID_BLOCK_ID;
    SymbolId symbol = INVALID_SYMBOL;
    StackSlotId stack_slot = INVALID_STACK_SLOT;
    std::uint32_t constant_pool_index = 0xFFFFFFFFu;
    std::uint32_t jump_table_index = 0xFFFFFFFFu;

    RelocationKind relocation = RelocationKind::None;

    std::uint32_t subregister = 0;
    SubRegIndex sub_reg_index = SubRegIndex::None;
    LaneBitmask lane_mask = kAllLanes;
    std::uint32_t flags = 0;

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool has_subregister() const noexcept {
        return sub_reg_index != SubRegIndex::None || subregister != 0;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] static MachineOperand use(MachineRegister r) noexcept {
        MachineOperand op;
        op.kind = MachineOperandKind::Register;
        op.role = OperandRole::Use;
        op.reg = r;
        return op;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] static MachineOperand def(MachineRegister r) noexcept {
        MachineOperand op;
        op.kind = MachineOperandKind::Register;
        op.role = OperandRole::Def;
        op.reg = r;
        return op;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] static MachineOperand imm(std::int64_t value) noexcept {
        MachineOperand op;
        op.kind = MachineOperandKind::Immediate;
        op.immediate = value;
        return op;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] static MachineOperand block_ref(BlockId id) noexcept {
        MachineOperand op;
        op.kind = MachineOperandKind::BasicBlock;
        op.block = id;
        return op;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] static MachineOperand stack_ref(StackSlotId id) noexcept {
        MachineOperand op;
        op.kind = MachineOperandKind::StackSlot;
        op.stack_slot = id;
        return op;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] static MachineOperand cpi(std::uint32_t idx) noexcept {
        MachineOperand op;
        op.kind = MachineOperandKind::ConstantPoolIndex;
        op.constant_pool_index = idx;
        return op;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] static MachineOperand jti(std::uint32_t idx) noexcept {
        MachineOperand op;
        op.kind = MachineOperandKind::JumpTableIndex;
        op.jump_table_index = idx;
        return op;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] static MachineOperand memory_ref(
        const MachineMemoryOperand& mem_op,
        OperandRole r = OperandRole::Use) noexcept {
        MachineOperand op;
        op.kind = MachineOperandKind::Memory;
        op.role = r;
        op.memory = mem_op;
        return op;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_register() const noexcept {
        return kind == MachineOperandKind::Register;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_memory() const noexcept {
        return kind == MachineOperandKind::Memory;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_immediate() const noexcept {
        return kind == MachineOperandKind::Immediate;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_def() const noexcept {
        return role == OperandRole::Def || role == OperandRole::UseDef ||
               role == OperandRole::ImplicitDef;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_use() const noexcept {
        return role == OperandRole::Use || role == OperandRole::UseDef ||
               role == OperandRole::ImplicitUse;
    }
};

/* ============================================================================
 * Industry-Grade Machine Opcode Architecture
 * ========================================================================== */

using MachineOpcode = std::uint16_t;

inline constexpr MachineOpcode INVALID_MACHINE_OPCODE = 0xFFFFu;
inline constexpr MachineOpcode GENERIC_OPCODE_START   = 0x0001u;
inline constexpr MachineOpcode GENERIC_OPCODE_END     = 0x7FFFu;
inline constexpr MachineOpcode TARGET_OPCODE_START    = 0x8000u;

enum GenericMachineOpcode : MachineOpcode {
    GM_INVALID = 0,
    GM_NOOP = 1,
    GM_COPY,
    GM_IMMEDIATE,
    GM_LOAD_STACK,
    GM_STORE_STACK,

    // Integer Binary 32-bit
    GM_IADD32, GM_ISUB32, GM_IMUL32, GM_IDIV32, GM_IREM32,
    GM_IAND32, GM_IOR32,  GM_IXOR32, GM_ISHL32, GM_ISHR32,

    // Integer Binary 64-bit
    GM_IADD64, GM_ISUB64, GM_IMUL64, GM_IDIV64, GM_IREM64,
    GM_IAND64, GM_IOR64,  GM_IXOR64, GM_ISHL64, GM_ISHR64,

    // Floating-Point
    GM_FADD32, GM_FSUB32, GM_FMUL32, GM_FDIV32,
    GM_FADD64, GM_FSUB64, GM_FMUL64, GM_FDIV64,
    GM_FFMA32, GM_FFMA64, GM_FAST_INV_SQRT,

    // Comparison & Control Flow
    GM_ICMP32, GM_ICMP64, GM_FCMP32, GM_FCMP64,
    GM_ICMP32_EQ, GM_ICMP32_NE, GM_ICMP32_LT, GM_ICMP32_LE, GM_ICMP32_GT, GM_ICMP32_GE,
    GM_ICMP64_EQ, GM_ICMP64_NE, GM_ICMP64_LT, GM_ICMP64_LE, GM_ICMP64_GT, GM_ICMP64_GE,
    GM_FCMP32_EQ, GM_FCMP32_NE, GM_FCMP32_LT, GM_FCMP32_LE, GM_FCMP32_GT, GM_FCMP32_GE,
    GM_FCMP64_EQ, GM_FCMP64_NE, GM_FCMP64_LT, GM_FCMP64_LE, GM_FCMP64_GT, GM_FCMP64_GE,
    GM_BR_COMPARE_EQ, GM_BR_COMPARE_NE, GM_BR_COMPARE_LT, GM_BR_COMPARE_LE, GM_BR_COMPARE_GT, GM_BR_COMPARE_GE,
    GM_GOTO, GM_BR_COND, GM_BR_COMPARE, GM_BR_TABLE,
    GM_CALL, GM_CALL_VARIADIC, GM_RETURN, GM_SYSCALL,

    // Memory
    GM_LOAD, GM_STORE, GM_VEC_LOAD, GM_VEC_STORE,
    GM_VEC_GATHER, GM_VEC_SCATTER,

    // Concurrency / Atomics
    GM_FENCE, GM_ACQUIRE_FENCE, GM_RELEASE_FENCE, GM_ATOMIC_FENCE,
    GM_ATOMIC_LOAD, GM_ATOMIC_STORE, GM_ATOMIC_SNAPSHOT,
    GM_LOCK_ACQUIRE, GM_LOCK_RELEASE, GM_SPIN_ACQUIRE,
    GM_BARRIER_SYNC, GM_SYNC_BARRIER,

    // Async / Coroutines / Fibers
    GM_ASYNC_AWAIT, GM_ASYNC_LAUNCH, GM_ASYNC_SPAWN,
    GM_FIBER_YIELD, GM_FIBER_YIELD_LOOP, GM_SUSPEND_CTX, GM_RESUME_CTX,
    GM_THREAD_SPAWN,

    // Memory & Address Math
    GM_LEA, GM_GEP, GM_PTR_DIFF, GM_PTR_TO_INT, GM_INT_TO_PTR,
    GM_PREFETCH, GM_PREFETCH_T0, GM_CLFLUSH, GM_READ_TSC,

    // String Primitives
    GM_STRING_LENGTH, GM_STRING_COMPARE, GM_STRING_CONCAT,
    GM_STRING_SUBSTRING, GM_STRING_COPY, GM_STRING_EQUALS,
    GM_STRING_FIND, GM_STRING_HASH,

    // Dynamic Allocations / Data Structures
    GM_DSA_ALLOCATE, GM_DSA_DEALLOCATE,
    GM_RING_PUSH_FREE, GM_DEOPT_TRIGGER,
    GM_TREE_BUILD, GM_TREE_TRAVERSE,
    GM_TRIE_BUILD, GM_TRIE_INSERT, GM_TRIE_LOOKUP,
    GM_GRAPH_BUILD, GM_GRAPH_TRAVERSE,
    GM_TUPLE_PACK, GM_TUPLE_UNPACK,

    // Tensor & Accelerator Kernels
    GM_TENSOR_OP, GM_TENSOR_BROADCAST, GM_TENSOR_CONCAT, GM_TENSOR_CONV,
    GM_TENSOR_GEMM, GM_TENSOR_GATHER, GM_TENSOR_POOL, GM_TENSOR_REDUCE,
    GM_TENSOR_RESHAPE, GM_TENSOR_SCATTER_D, GM_TENSOR_SLICE,
    GM_TENSOR_SOFTMAX, GM_TENSOR_TRANSPOSE,
    GM_MATMUL_BF16, GM_MATMUL_FP8, GM_SDPA_F16,
    GM_SOFTMAX_FAST, GM_LAYER_NORM, GM_ROPE_EMBED,
    GM_MAT4_MUL, GM_QUAT_SLERP, GM_VEC3_CROSS, GM_VEC4_DOT, GM_RAY_AABB_ISECT,
    GM_GPU_LAUNCH, GM_WASM_INVOKE,

    GM_MAX_GENERIC = GENERIC_OPCODE_END
};

[[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_generic_opcode(MachineOpcode op) noexcept {
    return op >= GENERIC_OPCODE_START && op <= GENERIC_OPCODE_END;
}

[[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_target_opcode(MachineOpcode op) noexcept {
    return op >= TARGET_OPCODE_START && op != INVALID_MACHINE_OPCODE;
}
 
/* ============================================================================
 * Comparison Predicates
 * ========================================================================== */

enum class ComparisonPredicate : std::uint8_t {
    Equal = 0,
    NotEqual = 1,
    SignedLessThan = 2,
    SignedLessEqual = 3,
    SignedGreaterThan = 4,
    SignedGreaterEqual = 5,
    UnsignedLessThan = 6,
    UnsignedLessEqual = 7,
    UnsignedGreaterThan = 8,
    UnsignedGreaterEqual = 9,
    Ordered = 10,
    Unordered = 11
};

inline constexpr std::string_view to_predicate_string(ComparisonPredicate pred) noexcept {
    switch (pred) {
        case ComparisonPredicate::Equal: return "eq";
        case ComparisonPredicate::NotEqual: return "ne";
        case ComparisonPredicate::SignedLessThan: return "lt";
        case ComparisonPredicate::SignedLessEqual: return "le";
        case ComparisonPredicate::SignedGreaterThan: return "gt";
        case ComparisonPredicate::SignedGreaterEqual: return "ge";
        case ComparisonPredicate::UnsignedLessThan: return "ult";
        case ComparisonPredicate::UnsignedLessEqual: return "ule";
        case ComparisonPredicate::UnsignedGreaterThan: return "ugt";
        case ComparisonPredicate::UnsignedGreaterEqual: return "uge";
        case ComparisonPredicate::Ordered: return "ord";
        case ComparisonPredicate::Unordered: return "uno";
        default: return "eq";
    }
}

/* ============================================================================
 * Canonical MachineForm and MachineSemanticKind
 * ========================================================================== */

using MachineForm = dgtl::mir::MachineForm;
using MachineSemanticKind = dgtl::mir::MachineSemanticKind;

[[nodiscard]] constexpr bool
semantic_kind_matches_form(MachineForm form, MachineSemanticKind kind) noexcept {
    return dgtl::mir::generated::semantic_kind_matches_form(form, kind);
}

[[nodiscard]] constexpr bool
requires_semantic_kind(MachineForm form) noexcept {
    return form != MachineForm::Unhandled;
}

enum class MachineInstrFlag : std::uint32_t {
    None            = 0,
    Terminator      = 1u << 0,
    Branch          = 1u << 1,
    Call            = 1u << 2,
    Return          = 1u << 3,
    Load            = 1u << 4,
    Store           = 1u << 5,
    Atomic          = 1u << 6,
    Barrier         = 1u << 7,
    SideEffect      = 1u << 8,
    Commutable      = 1u << 9,
    MayLoad         = 1u << 10,
    MayStore        = 1u << 11,
    HasUnmodeledSideEffect = 1u << 12,
    Pseudo          = 1u << 13,
    Debug           = 1u << 14,
    FrameSetup      = 1u << 15,
    FrameDestroy    = 1u << 16,
    CFI             = 1u << 17
};

constexpr MachineInstrFlag operator|(MachineInstrFlag a, MachineInstrFlag b) noexcept {
    return static_cast<MachineInstrFlag>(static_cast<std::uint32_t>(a) | static_cast<std::uint32_t>(b));
}

constexpr MachineInstrFlag operator&(MachineInstrFlag a, MachineInstrFlag b) noexcept {
    return static_cast<MachineInstrFlag>(static_cast<std::uint32_t>(a) & static_cast<std::uint32_t>(b));
}

enum class MachineTrait : std::uint64_t {
    None                = 0ULL,
    Commutative         = 1ULL << 0,
    HasNoSideEffects    = 1ULL << 1,
    IsTerminator        = 1ULL << 2,
    IsMemoryRead        = 1ULL << 3,
    IsMemoryWrite       = 1ULL << 4,
    IsMemoryOp          = 1ULL << 5,
    IsVectorizable      = 1ULL << 6,
    IsFusible           = 1ULL << 7,
    IsSpeculative       = 1ULL << 8,
    IsGuard             = 1ULL << 9,
    IsGPU               = 1ULL << 10,
    IsTensor            = 1ULL << 11,
    IsGraph             = 1ULL << 12,
    IsAsyncSpawnable    = 1ULL << 13,
    IsLatencyCritical   = 1ULL << 14,
    IsHardwareIntrinsic = 1ULL << 15,
    Associative         = 1ULL << 16,
    ConstantFoldable    = 1ULL << 17,
    MemoryBarrier       = 1ULL << 18,
    SingleDefUse        = 1ULL << 19,
    RequiresSpill       = 1ULL << 20,
    ClobbersFlags       = 1ULL << 21,
    TwoAddress          = 1ULL << 22,
    BranchDelaySlot     = 1ULL << 23
};

[[nodiscard]] constexpr MachineTrait operator|(MachineTrait a, MachineTrait b) noexcept {
    return static_cast<MachineTrait>(static_cast<std::uint64_t>(a) | static_cast<std::uint64_t>(b));
}
[[nodiscard]] constexpr MachineTrait operator&(MachineTrait a, MachineTrait b) noexcept {
    return static_cast<MachineTrait>(static_cast<std::uint64_t>(a) & static_cast<std::uint64_t>(b));
}
[[nodiscard]] constexpr MachineTrait operator~(MachineTrait a) noexcept {
    return static_cast<MachineTrait>(~static_cast<std::uint64_t>(a));
}

enum class MachineDomain : std::uint64_t {
    None        = 0ULL,
    Arithmetic  = 1ULL << 0,
    Memory      = 1ULL << 1,
    ControlFlow = 1ULL << 2,
    Terminator  = 1ULL << 3,
    Comparison  = 1ULL << 4,
    BitwiseOp   = 1ULL << 5,
    SIMD        = 1ULL << 6,
    GPU         = 1ULL << 7,
    Tensor      = 1ULL << 8,
    Concurrency = 1ULL << 9,
    DSA         = 1ULL << 10,
    Ownership   = 1ULL << 11,
    PointerMath = 1ULL << 12,
    NeuralNet   = 1ULL << 13,
    SpatialMath = 1ULL << 14
};

[[nodiscard]] constexpr MachineDomain operator|(MachineDomain a, MachineDomain b) noexcept {
    return static_cast<MachineDomain>(static_cast<std::uint64_t>(a) | static_cast<std::uint64_t>(b));
}
[[nodiscard]] constexpr MachineDomain operator&(MachineDomain a, MachineDomain b) noexcept {
    return static_cast<MachineDomain>(static_cast<std::uint64_t>(a) & static_cast<std::uint64_t>(b));
}

enum class MachineAttribute : std::uint64_t {
    None             = 0ULL,
    NoWrap           = 1ULL << 0,
    Exact            = 1ULL << 1,
    FastMath         = 1ULL << 2,
    Aligned          = 1ULL << 3,
    NoAlias          = 1ULL << 4,
    AddrSpaceGeneric = 1ULL << 5,
    Predicated       = 1ULL << 6,
    Relaxed          = 1ULL << 7,
    Acquire          = 1ULL << 8,
    Release          = 1ULL << 9,
    SeqCst           = 1ULL << 10,
    HotPath          = 1ULL << 11,
    ColdPath         = 1ULL << 12,
    LockFree         = 1ULL << 13,
    Variadic         = 1ULL << 14,
    MultiResult      = 1ULL << 15,
    Volatile         = 1ULL << 16
};

[[nodiscard]] constexpr MachineAttribute operator|(MachineAttribute a, MachineAttribute b) noexcept {
    return static_cast<MachineAttribute>(static_cast<std::uint64_t>(a) | static_cast<std::uint64_t>(b));
}
[[nodiscard]] constexpr MachineAttribute operator&(MachineAttribute a, MachineAttribute b) noexcept {
    return static_cast<MachineAttribute>(static_cast<std::uint64_t>(a) & static_cast<std::uint64_t>(b));
}

enum class MachineCFIKind : std::uint8_t {
    None = 0,
    DefCFA = 1,
    DefCFAOffset = 2,
    DefCFARegister = 3,
    Offset = 4,
    Restore = 5,
    RememberState = 6,
    RestoreState = 7
};

struct MachineCFIData {
    MachineCFIKind kind = MachineCFIKind::None;
    MachineRegister reg = {};
    std::int32_t offset = 0;
};

struct alignas(64) MachineInstruction {
    InstrId id = INVALID_INSTR_ID;
    MachineForm form = MachineForm::Unhandled;
    MachineSemanticKind semantic_kind = MachineSemanticKind::None;
    MachineOpcode opcode = INVALID_MACHINE_OPCODE;
    MachineInstrFlag flags = MachineInstrFlag::None;
    MachineCFIData cfi_data{};

    BlockId parent_block = INVALID_BLOCK_ID;
    InstrId prev_instr   = INVALID_INSTR_ID;
    InstrId next_instr   = INVALID_INSTR_ID;

    std::vector<MachineOperand> operands;

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_cfi_instruction() const noexcept {
        return cfi_data.kind != MachineCFIKind::None || (flags & MachineInstrFlag::CFI) != MachineInstrFlag::None;
    }

    std::uint32_t semantic_provenance = 0;
    std::uint32_t debug_id = 0;

    MachineTrait traits = MachineTrait::None;
    MachineDomain domains = MachineDomain::None;
    MachineAttribute attributes = MachineAttribute::None;
    std::uint64_t intent_hash = 0ULL;
    std::uint32_t epoch = 0;
    std::uint16_t latency_cycles = 1;
    std::uint16_t throughput_reciprocal = 1;

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool has_trait(MachineTrait t) const noexcept {
        return (traits & t) != MachineTrait::None;
    }

    void add_trait(MachineTrait t) noexcept {
        traits = traits | t;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool in_domain(MachineDomain d) const noexcept {
        return (domains & d) != MachineDomain::None;
    }

    void add_domain(MachineDomain d) noexcept {
        domains = domains | d;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool has_attribute(MachineAttribute a) const noexcept {
        return (attributes & a) != MachineAttribute::None;
    }

    void add_attribute(MachineAttribute a) noexcept {
        attributes = attributes | a;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool has_target_opcode() const noexcept {
        return is_target_opcode(opcode);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool has_opcode() const noexcept {
        return opcode != INVALID_MACHINE_OPCODE;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool assign_target_opcode_once(MachineOpcode target_opcode) noexcept {
        if (!is_target_opcode(target_opcode) || has_target_opcode()) {
            return false;
        }
        opcode = target_opcode;
        return true;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool has_semantic_kind() const noexcept {
        return semantic_kind != MachineSemanticKind::None;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool has_flag(MachineInstrFlag flag) const noexcept {
        return (flags & flag) != MachineInstrFlag::None;
    }

    void add_flag(MachineInstrFlag flag) noexcept {
        flags = flags | flag;
    }

    void add_operand(MachineOperand operand) {
        operands.push_back(std::move(operand));
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_terminator() const noexcept {
        return has_flag(MachineInstrFlag::Terminator);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_branch() const noexcept {
        return has_flag(MachineInstrFlag::Branch);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_call() const noexcept {
        return has_flag(MachineInstrFlag::Call);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_return() const noexcept {
        return has_flag(MachineInstrFlag::Return);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_load() const noexcept {
        return has_flag(MachineInstrFlag::Load);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_store() const noexcept {
        return has_flag(MachineInstrFlag::Store);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_barrier() const noexcept {
        return has_flag(MachineInstrFlag::Barrier);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_atomic() const noexcept {
        return has_flag(MachineInstrFlag::Atomic);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool may_load() const noexcept {
        return has_flag(MachineInstrFlag::MayLoad) || has_flag(MachineInstrFlag::Load);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool may_store() const noexcept {
        return has_flag(MachineInstrFlag::MayStore) || has_flag(MachineInstrFlag::Store);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool has_side_effects() const noexcept {
        return has_flag(MachineInstrFlag::SideEffect) || has_flag(MachineInstrFlag::HasUnmodeledSideEffect);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_commutable() const noexcept {
        return has_flag(MachineInstrFlag::Commutable);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_pseudo() const noexcept {
        return has_flag(MachineInstrFlag::Pseudo);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_frame_setup() const noexcept {
        return has_flag(MachineInstrFlag::FrameSetup);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_frame_destroy() const noexcept {
        return has_flag(MachineInstrFlag::FrameDestroy);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool is_phi() const noexcept {
        if (operands.size() >= 3 && (operands.size() % 2 == 1)) {
            if (operands[0].is_def()) {
                for (std::size_t i = 1; i < operands.size(); i += 2) {
                    if (operands[i].kind != MachineOperandKind::BasicBlock) {
                        return false;
                    }
                }
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] ComparisonPredicate comparison_predicate() const noexcept {
        for (const auto& op : operands) {
            if (op.kind == MachineOperandKind::Immediate && (op.flags & 0x80000000u)) {
                return static_cast<ComparisonPredicate>(op.immediate & 0xFF);
            }
        }
        switch (opcode) {
            case GM_ICMP32_EQ: case GM_ICMP64_EQ: case GM_FCMP32_EQ: case GM_FCMP64_EQ: case GM_BR_COMPARE_EQ:
                return ComparisonPredicate::Equal;
            case GM_ICMP32_NE: case GM_ICMP64_NE: case GM_FCMP32_NE: case GM_FCMP64_NE: case GM_BR_COMPARE_NE:
                return ComparisonPredicate::NotEqual;
            case GM_ICMP32_LT: case GM_ICMP64_LT: case GM_FCMP32_LT: case GM_FCMP64_LT: case GM_BR_COMPARE_LT:
                return ComparisonPredicate::SignedLessThan;
            case GM_ICMP32_LE: case GM_ICMP64_LE: case GM_FCMP32_LE: case GM_FCMP64_LE: case GM_BR_COMPARE_LE:
                return ComparisonPredicate::SignedLessEqual;
            case GM_ICMP32_GT: case GM_ICMP64_GT: case GM_FCMP32_GT: case GM_FCMP64_GT: case GM_BR_COMPARE_GT:
                return ComparisonPredicate::SignedGreaterThan;
            case GM_ICMP32_GE: case GM_ICMP64_GE: case GM_FCMP32_GE: case GM_FCMP64_GE: case GM_BR_COMPARE_GE:
                return ComparisonPredicate::SignedGreaterEqual;
            default: break;
        }
        if (semantic_provenance <= 11u) {
            return static_cast<ComparisonPredicate>(semantic_provenance);
        }
        switch (semantic_provenance) {
            case 140u: return ComparisonPredicate::Equal;
            case 141u: return ComparisonPredicate::NotEqual;
            case 142u: return ComparisonPredicate::SignedLessThan;
            case 143u: return ComparisonPredicate::SignedLessEqual;
            case 144u: return ComparisonPredicate::SignedGreaterThan;
            case 145u: return ComparisonPredicate::SignedGreaterEqual;
            default: return ComparisonPredicate::Equal;
        }
    }

    void set_comparison_predicate(ComparisonPredicate pred) noexcept {
        MachineOperand pred_op = MachineOperand::imm(static_cast<std::int64_t>(pred));
        pred_op.flags |= 0x80000000u;
        operands.push_back(pred_op);
        semantic_provenance = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(pred);
    }
};

} // namespace dgtl::machine

enum class MachineVerifyError : std::uint8_t {
    None,
    InvalidBlock,
    InvalidInstruction,
    InvalidOperand,
    InvalidRegister,
    MissingTerminator,
    InvalidCFGEdge,
    DuplicateBlock,
    DuplicateInstruction,
    InvalidStackSlot,
    InvalidMemoryOperand,
    InvalidOperandRole,
    MissingSemanticKind,
    MissingTargetOpcode,
    InvalidTargetOpcodeState,
    InvalidPhaseTransition,
    MissingEntryBlock,
    PhaseViolation,
    CFGIntegrityViolation,
    SSADominanceViolation
};

struct MachineVerifyResult {
    MachineVerifyError error = MachineVerifyError::None;
    BlockId block = INVALID_BLOCK_ID;
    InstrId instruction = INVALID_INSTR_ID;

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool ok() const noexcept {
        return error == MachineVerifyError::None;
    }
};

[[nodiscard]] constexpr dgtl::utils::BitMatrixLUT<16, 16> build_operand_role_legality_lut() noexcept {
    dgtl::utils::BitMatrixLUT<16, 16> lut{};
    for (std::size_t r = 0; r <= 7; ++r) {
        lut.set(static_cast<std::size_t>(MachineOperandKind::Register), r, true);
    }
    auto set_read_only = [&](MachineOperandKind k) {
        lut.set(static_cast<std::size_t>(k), static_cast<std::size_t>(OperandRole::Use), true);
        lut.set(static_cast<std::size_t>(k), static_cast<std::size_t>(OperandRole::Debug), true);
    };
    set_read_only(MachineOperandKind::Immediate);
    set_read_only(MachineOperandKind::FloatingImmediate);
    set_read_only(MachineOperandKind::BasicBlock);
    set_read_only(MachineOperandKind::Symbol);
    set_read_only(MachineOperandKind::StackSlot);
    set_read_only(MachineOperandKind::ConstantPoolIndex);
    set_read_only(MachineOperandKind::JumpTableIndex);
    set_read_only(MachineOperandKind::RegisterMask);
    set_read_only(MachineOperandKind::Metadata);
    set_read_only(MachineOperandKind::Memory);
    return lut;
}

inline constexpr auto kOperandRoleLegalityLUT = build_operand_role_legality_lut();

inline bool MachineFunction::verify_cfg_integrity(std::string* out_error) const {
    const BlockId root = entry_block();
    if (root != INVALID_BLOCK_ID && root >= blocks().size()) {
        if (out_error) *out_error = "Entry block ID is out of range.";
        return false;
    }
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < blocks().size(); ++i) {
        const auto* bb_ptr = block(static_cast<BlockId>(i));
        if (!bb_ptr) continue;
        const auto& bb = *bb_ptr;
        if (bb.id != static_cast<BlockId>(i)) {
            if (out_error) *out_error = "Block id does not match vector index at " + std::to_string(i);
            return false;
        }
        for (BlockId succ_id : bb.successors) {
            if (succ_id >= blocks().size()) {
                if (out_error) *out_error = "Successor " + std::to_string(succ_id) + " out of range in block " + std::to_string(bb.id);
                return false;
            }
            const auto* succ_bb = block(succ_id);
            if (!succ_bb || std::find(succ_bb->predecessors.begin(), succ_bb->predecessors.end(), bb.id) == succ_bb->predecessors.end()) {
                if (out_error) *out_error = "CFG asymmetry: block " + std::to_string(bb.id) + " has successor " + std::to_string(succ_id) + " but successor lacks predecessor back-edge.";
                return false;
            }
        }
        for (BlockId pred_id : bb.predecessors) {
            if (pred_id >= blocks().size()) {
                if (out_error) *out_error = "Predecessor " + std::to_string(pred_id) + " out of range in block " + std::to_string(bb.id);
                return false;
            }
            const auto* pred_bb = block(pred_id);
            if (!pred_bb || std::find(pred_bb->successors.begin(), pred_bb->successors.end(), bb.id) == pred_bb->successors.end()) {
                if (out_error) *out_error = "CFG asymmetry: block " + std::to_string(bb.id) + " has predecessor " + std::to_string(pred_id) + " but predecessor lacks successor forward-edge.";
                return false;
            }
        }
        InstrId curr = bb.head_instr;
        InstrId prev = INVALID_INSTR_ID;
        std::size_t counted = 0;
        while (curr != INVALID_INSTR_ID) {
            if (curr >= instructions().size()) {
                if (out_error) *out_error = "Instruction ID " + std::to_string(curr) + " out of range in block " + std::to_string(bb.id);
                return false;
            }
            const auto* inst = instruction(curr);
            if (!inst || inst->parent_block != bb.id) {
                if (out_error) *out_error = "Instruction " + std::to_string(curr) + " parent_block mismatch in block " + std::to_string(bb.id);
                return false;
            }
            if (inst->prev_instr != prev) {
                if (out_error) *out_error = "Instruction chain broken: prev_instr mismatch at instr " + std::to_string(curr);
                return false;
            }
            prev = curr;
            curr = inst->next_instr;
            counted++;
            if (counted > instructions().size()) {
                if (out_error) *out_error = "Cycle detected in instruction intrusive list of block " + std::to_string(bb.id);
                return false;
            }
        }
        if (prev != bb.tail_instr) {
            if (out_error) *out_error = "Tail instruction pointer mismatch in block " + std::to_string(bb.id);
            return false;
        }
        if (counted != bb.instructions.size()) {
            if (out_error) *out_error = "Intrusive chain count " + std::to_string(counted) + " does not match instructions vector size " + std::to_string(bb.instructions.size()) + " in block " + std::to_string(bb.id);
            return false;
        }
    }
    return true;
}

inline bool MachineFunction::verify_ssa_dominance(std::string* out_error) const {
    if (!properties().is_ssa) {
        return true;
    }

    struct DefLocation {
        InstrId instr_id = INVALID_INSTR_ID;
        BlockId block_id = INVALID_BLOCK_ID;
        std::size_t instr_index_in_block = 0;
    };

    std::unordered_map<RegId, DefLocation> vreg_defs;

    for (const auto& bb : blocks()) {
        for (std::size_t idx = 0; idx < bb.instructions.size(); ++idx) {
            const InstrId iid = bb.instructions[idx];
            const auto* inst = instruction(iid);
            if (!inst) continue;

            for (const auto& op : inst->operands) {
                if (op.is_register() && op.is_def() && op.reg.is_virtual()) {
                    const RegId r = op.reg.id;
                    if (vreg_defs.find(r) != vreg_defs.end()) {
                        if (out_error) {
                            *out_error = "SSA violation: virtual register v" + std::to_string(r) +
                                         " defined multiple times (first in instr " +
                                         std::to_string(vreg_defs[r].instr_id) + ", then in instr " +
                                         std::to_string(iid) + ").";
                        }
                        return false;
                    }
                    vreg_defs[r] = DefLocation{iid, bb.id, idx};
                }
            }
        }
    }

    const auto& dom = dominators();

    for (const auto& bb : blocks()) {
        for (std::size_t idx = 0; idx < bb.instructions.size(); ++idx) {
            const InstrId iid = bb.instructions[idx];
            const auto* inst = instruction(iid);
            if (!inst) continue;

            const bool is_phi = inst->is_phi();

            if (is_phi) {
                for (std::size_t op_idx = 1; op_idx + 1 < inst->operands.size(); op_idx += 2) {
                    const auto& block_op = inst->operands[op_idx];
                    const auto& val_op = inst->operands[op_idx + 1];
                    if (block_op.kind == MachineOperandKind::BasicBlock &&
                        val_op.is_register() && val_op.reg.is_virtual()) {
                        const RegId r = val_op.reg.id;
                        auto def_it = vreg_defs.find(r);
                        if (def_it == vreg_defs.end()) {
                            if (out_error) {
                                *out_error = "SSA use without def: virtual register v" + std::to_string(r) +
                                             " used in PHI instruction " + std::to_string(iid) + " has no definition.";
                            }
                            return false;
                        }
                        const BlockId pred_block = block_op.block;
                        const DefLocation& def_loc = def_it->second;
                        if (!dom.dominates(def_loc.block_id, pred_block)) {
                            if (out_error) {
                                *out_error = "SSA dominance violation: definition of v" + std::to_string(r) +
                                             " in block " + std::to_string(def_loc.block_id) +
                                             " does not dominate PHI incoming predecessor block " +
                                             std::to_string(pred_block) + " in block " + std::to_string(bb.id);
                            }
                            return false;
                        }
                    }
                }
            } else {
                for (const auto& op : inst->operands) {
                    if (op.is_register() && op.is_use() && op.reg.is_virtual()) {
                        const RegId r = op.reg.id;
                        auto def_it = vreg_defs.find(r);
                        if (def_it == vreg_defs.end()) {
                            if (out_error) {
                                *out_error = "SSA use without def: virtual register v" + std::to_string(r) +
                                             " used in instruction " + std::to_string(iid) + " has no definition.";
                            }
                            return false;
                        }
                        const DefLocation& def_loc = def_it->second;
                        if (def_loc.block_id == bb.id) {
                            if (def_loc.instr_index_in_block >= idx) {
                                if (out_error) {
                                    *out_error = "SSA intra-block dominance violation: use of v" + std::to_string(r) +
                                                 " at instr " + std::to_string(iid) + " precedes its definition at instr " +
                                                 std::to_string(def_loc.instr_id);
                                }
                                return false;
                            }
                        } else {
                            if (!dom.dominates(def_loc.block_id, bb.id)) {
                                if (out_error) {
                                    *out_error = "SSA dominance violation: definition of v" + std::to_string(r) +
                                                 " in block " + std::to_string(def_loc.block_id) +
                                                 " does not dominate use block " + std::to_string(bb.id) +
                                                 " at instr " + std::to_string(iid);
                                }
                                return false;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    return true;
}

inline BlockBranchTopology MachineFunction::block_branch_topology(BlockId block_id) const noexcept {
    const auto* bb = block(block_id);
    if (!bb) return BlockBranchTopology::Malformed;
    if (bb->instructions.empty() || bb->tail_instr == INVALID_INSTR_ID) {
        return BlockBranchTopology::EmptyOrFallthrough;
    }
    const auto* tail = instruction(bb->tail_instr);
    if (!tail) return BlockBranchTopology::Malformed;

    if (!tail->has_flag(MachineInstrFlag::Terminator)) {
        return BlockBranchTopology::EmptyOrFallthrough;
    }

    if (tail->form == MachineForm::Return || tail->has_flag(MachineInstrFlag::Return)) {
        return BlockBranchTopology::Return;
    }
    if (tail->form == MachineForm::Syscall) {
        return BlockBranchTopology::SyscallOrTrap;
    }
    if (tail->form == MachineForm::Goto) {
        if (tail->prev_instr != INVALID_INSTR_ID) {
            const auto* prev = instruction(tail->prev_instr);
            if (prev && (prev->form == MachineForm::BranchCond || prev->form == MachineForm::BranchCompare)) {
                return BlockBranchTopology::ConditionalAndUnconditionalBranch;
            }
        }
        return BlockBranchTopology::UnconditionalBranch;
    }
    if (tail->form == MachineForm::BranchCond || tail->form == MachineForm::BranchCompare) {
        return BlockBranchTopology::ConditionalBranchWithFallthrough;
    }
    for (const auto& op : tail->operands) {
        if (op.kind == MachineOperandKind::JumpTableIndex) {
            return BlockBranchTopology::IndirectBranch;
        }
    }
    return BlockBranchTopology::UnconditionalBranch;
}

class MachineVerifier {
public:
    [[nodiscard]] static MachineVerifyResult
    verify(const MachineFunction& function) noexcept {
        if (!function.verify_cfg_integrity()) {
            return {MachineVerifyError::CFGIntegrityViolation, INVALID_BLOCK_ID, INVALID_INSTR_ID};
        }
        if (function.properties().is_ssa && !function.verify_ssa_dominance()) {
            return {MachineVerifyError::SSADominanceViolation, INVALID_BLOCK_ID, INVALID_INSTR_ID};
        }

        const auto blocks = function.blocks();
        const auto instructions = function.instructions();

        std::vector<std::uint32_t> instruction_membership(instructions.size(), 0u);

        if (function.is_phase_at_least(MachinePhase::PreRegAlloc)) {
            if (blocks.empty() || function.entry_block() == INVALID_BLOCK_ID ||
                function.entry_block() >= blocks.size() ||
                !blocks[function.entry_block()].entry) {
                return {MachineVerifyError::MissingEntryBlock,
                        INVALID_BLOCK_ID, INVALID_INSTR_ID};
            }
        }

        for (std::size_t i = 0; i < blocks.size(); ++i) {
            if (blocks[i].id != static_cast<BlockId>(i)) {
                return {
                    MachineVerifyError::DuplicateBlock,
                    blocks[i].id,
                    INVALID_INSTR_ID
                };
            }

            if (blocks[i].instr_count != blocks[i].instructions.size()) {
                return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidInstruction,
                        blocks[i].id, INVALID_INSTR_ID};
            }
            if (blocks[i].instructions.empty()) {
                if (blocks[i].head_instr != INVALID_INSTR_ID ||
                    blocks[i].tail_instr != INVALID_INSTR_ID) {
                    return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidInstruction,
                            blocks[i].id, INVALID_INSTR_ID};
                }
            } else if (blocks[i].head_instr == INVALID_INSTR_ID ||
                       blocks[i].tail_instr == INVALID_INSTR_ID) {
                return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidInstruction,
                        blocks[i].id, INVALID_INSTR_ID};
            }

            bool seen_terminator = false;
            for (std::size_t position = 0; position < blocks[i].instructions.size(); ++position) {
                const InstrId id = blocks[i].instructions[position];
                if (id >= instructions.size()) {
                    return {
                        MachineVerifyError::InvalidInstruction,
                        blocks[i].id,
                        id
                    };
                }
                if (++instruction_membership[id] != 1u) {
                    return {
                        MachineVerifyError::DuplicateInstruction,
                        blocks[i].id,
                        id
                    };
                }

                const MachineInstruction& block_instr = instructions[id];

                if (block_instr.parent_block != blocks[i].id) {
                    return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidInstruction, blocks[i].id, id};
                }

                if (block_instr.form == MachineForm::Unhandled) {
                    return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidInstruction, blocks[i].id, id};
                }

                if (requires_semantic_kind(block_instr.form) &&
                    !block_instr.has_semantic_kind()) {
                    return {MachineVerifyError::MissingSemanticKind, blocks[i].id, id};
                }
                if (block_instr.has_semantic_kind() &&
                    !semantic_kind_matches_form(block_instr.form, block_instr.semantic_kind)) {
                    return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidInstruction, blocks[i].id, id};
                }

                const bool emission_phase =
                    function.is_phase_at_least(MachinePhase::FrameLowered);
                if (emission_phase && !block_instr.has_target_opcode()) {
                    return {MachineVerifyError::MissingTargetOpcode, blocks[i].id, id};
                }

                if (seen_terminator) {
                    return {
                        MachineVerifyError::InvalidInstruction,
                        blocks[i].id,
                        id
                    };
                }
                if (block_instr.has_flag(MachineInstrFlag::Terminator)) {
                    seen_terminator = true;
                    if (position + 1u != blocks[i].instructions.size()) {
                        return {
                            MachineVerifyError::InvalidInstruction,
                            blocks[i].id,
                            id
                        };
                    }
                }

                for (const MachineOperand& operand : block_instr.operands) {
                    switch (operand.kind) {
                    case MachineOperandKind::Register:
                        if (!operand.reg.valid() ||
                            operand.reg.reg_class == RegisterClassKind::Invalid) {
                            return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidRegister, blocks[i].id, id};
                        }
                        break;
                    case MachineOperandKind::BasicBlock:
                        if (operand.block == INVALID_BLOCK_ID || operand.block >= blocks.size()) {
                            return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidOperand, blocks[i].id, id};
                        }
                        break;
                    case MachineOperandKind::StackSlot:
                        if (operand.stack_slot == INVALID_STACK_SLOT ||
                            operand.stack_slot >= function.frame_info().slots.size()) {
                            return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidStackSlot, blocks[i].id, id};
                        }
                        break;
                    case MachineOperandKind::ConstantPoolIndex:
                        if (operand.constant_pool_index >= function.constant_pool().entries().size()) {
                            return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidOperand, blocks[i].id, id};
                        }
                        break;
                    case MachineOperandKind::JumpTableIndex:
                        if (operand.jump_table_index >= function.jump_table_info().entries().size()) {
                            return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidOperand, blocks[i].id, id};
                        }
                        break;
                    case MachineOperandKind::Memory:
                        if (operand.memory.scale != 1 && operand.memory.scale != 2 &&
                            operand.memory.scale != 4 && operand.memory.scale != 8) {
                            return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidMemoryOperand, blocks[i].id, id};
                        }
                        if (operand.memory.alignment == 0) {
                            return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidMemoryOperand, blocks[i].id, id};
                        }
                        if (!operand.memory.load && !operand.memory.store &&
                            (block_instr.has_flag(MachineInstrFlag::Load) ||
                             block_instr.has_flag(MachineInstrFlag::Store))) {
                            return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidMemoryOperand, blocks[i].id, id};
                        }
                        break;
                    case MachineOperandKind::Invalid:
                        return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidOperand, blocks[i].id, id};
                    default:
                        break;
                    }

                    if (!kOperandRoleLegalityLUT.get(static_cast<std::size_t>(operand.kind),
                                                     static_cast<std::size_t>(operand.role))) {
                        return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidOperandRole, blocks[i].id, id};
                    }
                }
            }

            if (function.is_phase_at_least(MachinePhase::PreRegAlloc) &&
                !blocks[i].instructions.empty() && !seen_terminator) {
                return {MachineVerifyError::MissingTerminator,
                        blocks[i].id, INVALID_INSTR_ID};
            }

            for (BlockId successor : blocks[i].successors) {
                if (successor >= blocks.size()) {
                    return {
                        MachineVerifyError::InvalidCFGEdge,
                        blocks[i].id,
                        INVALID_INSTR_ID
                    };
                }
            }

            for (BlockId predecessor : blocks[i].predecessors) {
                if (predecessor >= blocks.size()) {
                    return {
                        MachineVerifyError::InvalidCFGEdge,
                        blocks[i].id,
                        INVALID_INSTR_ID
                    };
                }
            }
        }

        for (const MachineBasicBlock& block : blocks) {
            InstrId cursor = block.head_instr;
            InstrId previous = INVALID_INSTR_ID;
            std::size_t chain_count = 0;
            while (cursor != INVALID_INSTR_ID) {
                if (cursor >= instructions.size() || chain_count > block.instructions.size()) {
                    return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidInstruction,
                            block.id, cursor};
                }
                const MachineInstruction& chained = instructions[cursor];
                if (chained.parent_block != block.id ||
                    chained.prev_instr != previous) {
                    return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidInstruction,
                            block.id, cursor};
                }
                if (std::find(block.instructions.begin(),
                              block.instructions.end(), cursor) == block.instructions.end()) {
                    return {MachineVerifyError::DuplicateInstruction,
                            block.id, cursor};
                }
                previous = cursor;
                cursor = chained.next_instr;
                ++chain_count;
            }
            if (chain_count != block.instructions.size() ||
                previous != block.tail_instr) {
                return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidInstruction,
                        block.id, INVALID_INSTR_ID};
            }
            if (block.tail_instr != INVALID_INSTR_ID &&
                instructions[block.tail_instr].next_instr != INVALID_INSTR_ID) {
                return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidInstruction,
                        block.id, block.tail_instr};
            }

            for (BlockId successor : block.successors) {
                const MachineBasicBlock& target = blocks[successor];
                if (std::find(target.predecessors.begin(),
                              target.predecessors.end(),
                              block.id) == target.predecessors.end()) {
                    return {
                        MachineVerifyError::InvalidCFGEdge,
                        block.id,
                        INVALID_INSTR_ID
                    };
                }
            }
            for (BlockId predecessor : block.predecessors) {
                const MachineBasicBlock& source = blocks[predecessor];
                if (std::find(source.successors.begin(),
                              source.successors.end(),
                              block.id) == source.successors.end()) {
                    return {
                        MachineVerifyError::InvalidCFGEdge,
                        block.id,
                        INVALID_INSTR_ID
                    };
                }
            }
        }

        for (std::size_t i = 0; i < instruction_membership.size(); ++i) {
            if (instruction_membership[i] != 1u) {
                return {
                    MachineVerifyError::DuplicateInstruction,
                    INVALID_BLOCK_ID,
                    static_cast<InstrId>(i)
                };
            }
        }

        for (const MachineInstruction& instr : instructions) {
            if (instr.id == INVALID_INSTR_ID ||
                instr.form == MachineForm::Unhandled) {
                return {
                    MachineVerifyError::InvalidInstruction,
                    INVALID_BLOCK_ID,
                    instr.id
                };
            }

            const bool before_target_lowering =
                static_cast<std::uint8_t>(function.phase()) <
                static_cast<std::uint8_t>(MachinePhase::PreRegAlloc);
            if (before_target_lowering) {
                if (is_target_opcode(instr.opcode)) {
                    return {
                        MachineVerifyError::PhaseViolation,
                        INVALID_BLOCK_ID,
                        instr.id
                    };
                }
            } else {
                if (!instr.has_target_opcode()) {
                    return {
                        MachineVerifyError::MissingTargetOpcode,
                        INVALID_BLOCK_ID,
                        instr.id
                    };
                }
            }

            for (const MachineOperand& operand : instr.operands) {
                if (operand.kind == MachineOperandKind::Register &&
                    !operand.reg.valid()) {
                    return {
                        MachineVerifyError::InvalidRegister,
                        INVALID_BLOCK_ID,
                        instr.id
                    };
                }

                if (operand.kind == MachineOperandKind::BasicBlock &&
                    operand.block >= blocks.size()) {
                    return {
                        MachineVerifyError::InvalidBlock,
                        operand.block,
                        instr.id
                    };
                }

                if (operand.kind == MachineOperandKind::StackSlot &&
                    operand.stack_slot >= function.frame_info().slots.size()) {
                    return {
                        MachineVerifyError::InvalidStackSlot,
                        INVALID_BLOCK_ID,
                        instr.id
                    };
                }
            }
        }

        for (const MachineStackSlot& slot : function.frame_info().slots) {
            if (slot.id == INVALID_STACK_SLOT) {
                return {
                    MachineVerifyError::InvalidStackSlot,
                    INVALID_BLOCK_ID,
                    INVALID_INSTR_ID
                };
            }
            if (slot.alignment == 0u || slot.size_bytes == 0u) {
                return {
                    MachineVerifyError::InvalidStackSlot,
                    INVALID_BLOCK_ID,
                    INVALID_INSTR_ID
                };
            }
        }

        if (static_cast<std::uint8_t>(function.phase()) >=
            static_cast<std::uint8_t>(MachinePhase::PostRegAlloc)) {
            if (function.has_virtual_registers() ||
                !function.properties().registers_allocated) {
                return {
                    MachineVerifyError::InvalidRegister,
                    INVALID_BLOCK_ID,
                    INVALID_INSTR_ID
                };
            }
        }

        if (static_cast<std::uint8_t>(function.phase()) >=
            static_cast<std::uint8_t>(MachinePhase::FrameLowered)) {
            const auto& frame = function.frame_info();

            if (frame.stack_alignment == 0u) {
                return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidStackSlot,
                        INVALID_BLOCK_ID, INVALID_INSTR_ID};
            }

            for (const auto& slot : frame.slots) {
                if (!slot.fixed_offset || slot.alignment == 0u ||
                    slot.size_bytes == 0u) {
                    return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidStackSlot,
                            INVALID_BLOCK_ID, INVALID_INSTR_ID};
                }
                const auto alignment = static_cast<std::int64_t>(slot.alignment);
                if (slot.offset % alignment != 0) {
                    return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidStackSlot,
                            INVALID_BLOCK_ID, INVALID_INSTR_ID};
                }
            }
        }

        if (function.is_phase_at_least(MachinePhase::EmissionReady)) {
            if (!function.has_target_opcodes() || function.has_virtual_registers()) {
                return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidTargetOpcodeState,
                        INVALID_BLOCK_ID, INVALID_INSTR_ID};
            }
            if (!function.properties().frame_lowered ||
                !function.properties().abi_lowered ||
                !function.properties().registers_allocated) {
                return {MachineVerifyError::PhaseViolation,
                        INVALID_BLOCK_ID, INVALID_INSTR_ID};
            }
        }

        return {};
    }
};

} // namespace dgtl::machine

FILE: include\dgtl\mir\machine_ir\Machine_Op_Wrappers.ixx

 *
